# PART 06 — TONE 6: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*[Engraved Headpiece: An ornate multi-panel baroque frieze depicting the Prophet Moses with the Tablets of the Law, King David, the Prophet Isaiah, the Holy Theotokos holding the Christ Child, Saint John the Baptist, and Saint John Chrysostom, framed in arabesque cartouches]*

*On Saturday Evening.*
*At Little Vespers.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 4 verses, and sing 3 Resurrection Stichera, repeating the first.*
*Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* Having victory over hades, O Christ, Thou didst ascend the Cross, that Thou mightest raise with Thyself those sitting in the darkness of death, O Free among the dead: O Fountain of life from Thine own light, all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us. *(Twice)*[^6519]

*Sticheron 2 (Resurrectional):* Today Christ, having trampled down death, rose even as He said, and bestowed joy unto the world, that we all, crying aloud a song, may say thus: O Fountain of life, Unapproachable Light, all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us.[^6520]

*Sticheron 3 (Resurrectional):* Whither shall we sinners flee from Thee, O Lord, Who fillest all creation?[^6521] In heaven Thou Thyself dwellest; in hades Thou hast trampled down death; into the depths of the sea? there is Thy hand, O Master. Unto Thee do we flee, falling down before Thee we pray: O Thou Who art risen from the dead, have mercy on us.[^6522]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Come, all ye nations, let us praise with the voice of rejoicing the most holy Virgin and Mother of God, the crucible of human nature, the worker of ineffable wonders; for in her new things have come to pass: the Unoriginate begins, the Word takes on flesh, God becomes man, that He may make man God, not by a change of natures, but by union according to hypostasis; for there proceeds One from two opposites, in two perfect natures indivisibly made known, in will and in energy according to each essence. Being Himself believed according to His true reality, for the sake of saving dispensation, Christ our God, granting unto the world cleansing, peace, and great mercy.[^6523]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon: "The Lord is King," three times. Vouchsafe, O Lord.*[^6524]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Sticheron, Tone 6:*
Thy Resurrection, O Christ Savior, the angels hymn in the heavens: and account us upon earth worthy to glorify Thee with a pure heart.[^6525]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Всѐ возло́жше):*
*Verse: I will remember thy name in every generation and generation.*[^6526]
Having given birth unto the incarnate Word, Who is co-eternal with the Father before the ages, O all-immaculate Virgin...

...loose the bonds of my humble soul, as I cry unto thee, the Helper of the world: deliver me from the dominion of passions and from sinful calamities; and I pray thee, save me, O All-Immaculate One, from the snares of the alien, for the sake of thy great mercy.[^6527]

*Verse: Give ear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear.*[^6528]
*Sticheron 2:* Save me who am in despair through fornications, stripped bare of good works, and alienated from the Master's grace, O thou who alone didst bear the salvation of the world: deliver from perils and captivity me who willfully surrendered myself, and who through senselessness cast myself into the pit of destruction, O Lady, as thou art merciful and full of love for souls.[^6529]

*Verse: Virgins shall be brought unto the King after her, her companions shall be brought unto Thee.*[^6530]
*Sticheron 3:* Look upon my affliction, O Mother of God, and give ear unto the prayer of thy petitioner, O Pure One; grant me the mighty hand of thy help, O Mother of God! Receive the groaning from a defiled heart, O Most Holy One, and bear it unto thy Son and our God, O blessed Lady, for the sake of thy great mercy.[^6531]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion:*
It is truly meet to call thee blessed, O Mother of God: for entering into thy most pure womb, the Maker of all became flesh, not being changed in nature, nor imagining the dispensation; but being united according to hypostasis unto the rationally ensouled flesh taken from thee; whence piously we make distinction of those things manifested in both natures. Him do thou entreat, O honorable and most holy Lady, to send down upon us peace and great mercy.[^6532]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart; Trisagion, and after Our Father: Troparion: "The angelic powers were upon Thy tomb"; Theotokion: "He that called Thy Mother blessed"; litany, and dismissal.*[^6533]

*On Saturday, at Great Vespers.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and sing the following Resurrection Stichera, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* Having victory over hades, O Christ, Thou didst ascend the Cross, that Thou mightest raise with Thyself those sitting in the darkness of death, O Free among the dead: O Fountain of life from Thine own light, all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us.[^6534]

*Sticheron 2 (Resurrectional):* Today Christ, having trampled down death, rose even as He said, and bestowed joy unto the world, that we all, crying aloud, may say a song thus: O Fountain of life, Unapproachable Light, all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us.[^6535]

*Sticheron 3 (Resurrectional):* Whither shall we sinners flee from Thee, O Lord, Who fillest all creation?[^6536] In heaven Thou Thyself dwellest; in hades Thou hast trampled down death; into the depths of the sea? there is Thy hand, O Master. Unto Thee do we flee, falling down before Thee we pray: O Thou Who art risen from the dead, have mercy on us.[^6537]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian (Eastern), the same Tone:*
*Sticheron 4 (Anatolian):* In Thy Cross, O Christ, do we boast, and Thy Resurrection we hymn and glorify; for Thou art our God, besides Thee we know no other.[^6538]

*Sticheron 5 (Anatolian):* Glory to Thy power, O Lord; for Thou hast brought to naught him that had the dominion of death; Thou hast renewed us by Thy Cross: grant unto us life and incorruption.[^6539]

*Sticheron 6 (Anatolian):* Ever blessing the Lord, we hymn His Resurrection; for having endured the Cross, by death He destroyed death.[^6540]

*Sticheron 7 (Anatolian):* Thy burial, O Lord, having broken in pieces the bonds of hades, tore them asunder; Thy Resurrection from the dead enlightened the world: O Lord, glory to Thee.[^6541]

*Other Stichera, to the Theotokos (The composition of Paul of Amorion), Tone 6:*
*Sticheron 8 (By Paul of Amorion):* Seeing my bodily weakness, and the suffering of my soul, and the affliction of my heart, account me worthy of Thy divine...

...visitation, O all-immaculate Virgin! Save me, I pray thee, by thy fervent supplications.[^6542]

*Sticheron 9 (By Paul of Amorion):* I have surpassed all in sins, O Lady; but having cleansed the multitude of them, O Virgin, account me worthy to obtain mercies, O Pure One, at the future judgment of thy Son and God.[^6543]

*Sticheron 10 (By Paul of Amorion):* Cleanse the multitude of my transgressions, O Pure One, who cry unto thee, and cut down with the sword of prayer the unbridled movements of my senses, that with faith and love I may hymn thy seedless childbirth.[^6544]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion:*
Who doth not call thee blessed, O all-holy Virgin? Who will not sing of thy most pure childbirth? For the only-begotten Son, Who shone timelessly from the Father, the same came forth from thee, the Pure One, ineffably incarnate: being God by nature, and having become man by nature for our sake; not divided into two persons, but known in two natures without confusion. Him do thou entreat, O pure and all-blessed one, that our souls may find mercy.[^6545]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon: "The Lord is King," and the rest.*[^6546]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 6:*
*Resurrection Sticheron:* Thy Resurrection, O Christ Savior, the angels hymn in the heavens: and account us upon earth worthy to glorify Thee with a pure heart.[^6547]

*(Alphabetical Stichera):*
*Verse: The Lord is King, He is clothed with majesty.*[^6548]
*Sticheron 1 (Alphabetical):* Having broken in pieces the gates of brass, and shattered the bars of hades as almighty God, Thou didst raise up the fallen human race: for this reason we also cry out with one accord: O Lord Who art risen from the dead, glory to Thee.[^6549]

*Verse: For He hath established the world, which shall not be shaken.*[^6550]
*Sticheron 2 (Alphabetical):* Desiring to right us from ancient corruption, Christ was nailed upon the Cross and laid in a tomb; Whom the myrrh-bearing women sought with tears, weeping and saying: Woe unto us, O Savior of all! How didst Thou deign to take up Thine abode in a tomb?[^6551] And having taken up Thine abode willingly, how wast Thou stolen away? How hast Thou been translated? What place hid Thy life-bearing body? But O Master, even as Thou didst promise, appear unto us, and soothe from us the weeping of tears! And while they were weeping, an angel cried unto them: Ceasing from weeping, say unto the Apostles that the Lord is risen, granting unto the world cleansing and great mercy.[^6552]

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thy house, O Lord, unto length of days.*[^6553]
*Sticheron 3 (Alphabetical):* Being crucified as Thou didst will, O Christ, and having despoiled death by Thy burial, Thou didst rise on the third day as God with glory: granting unto the world unending life and great mercy.[^6554]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* My Creator and Redeemer, O All-Pure One, Christ the Lord, coming forth from thy womb, clothed in me, delivered Adam from the first curse; therefore unto thee, O All-Immaculate One, as in truth the Mother of God and Virgin, we cry unceasingly with the angel: Rejoice! Rejoice, O Lady, protection and shelter, and salvation of our souls.[^6555]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart; Trisagion, and after Our Father:*
*Resurrection Troparion, Tone 6:*
The angelic powers were upon Thy tomb, and the guards became as dead men; and Mary stood at the tomb, seeking Thy most pure body. Thou didst despoil hades, not being tempted by it; Thou didst meet the Virgin, granting life: O Lord Who art risen from the dead, glory to Thee.[^6556]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Calling Thy Mother blessed, Thou camest unto the Passion by voluntary will, shining upon the Cross, desiring to seek out Adam, saying unto the angels: Rejoice with Me, for the lost drachma hath been found.[^6557] O our God Who hast wisely ordered all things, glory to Thee.[^6558]

*Thereafter, the dismissal.*[^6559]

*On Saturday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 6.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having walked on foot upon the deep with dryshod steps as upon dry land, Israel, beholding Pharaoh the pursuer drowned, cried: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^6560]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^6561]
*Troparion 1:* Having unstinting grace as divine, O unwedded Mother, despise not those who flee unto thee, ever saving them from perils and afflictions.[^6562]

*Troparion 2:* Behold, the time of my despondency is come: arise, O all-pure Lady, and give me a hand of help; for thou didst fill the world with divine joy.[^6563]

*Glory:* Being my protection, O Mother of God, grant speedily thy mighty protection unto me who am held fast by temptations, and preserve me unharmed by them.[^6564]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Death draws near unto my soul, and the temptations of those that hate me have surrounded me like asps to destroy me; but do thou thyself save me, O Mother of God.[^6565]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* My mouth hath opened wide against mine enemies, for my heart is established in the Lord.[^6566]

*Troparion 1:* My soul that is deadened by transgressions, O Mother of God who didst bear the Life, do thou quicken.[^6567]

*Troparion 2:* From every temptation preserve those who flee unto thee, O Mother of God, our hope.[^6568]

*Glory:* O all-immaculate Mother of my Lord, bitterly save me from the afflictions that rise up against me.[^6569]

*Both now... Theotokion:* O thou who grantest salvation unto the world through thy divine Nativity, deliver me from perils.[^6570]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* The Prophet heard of Thy coming, O Lord, and was afraid, that Thou wast about to be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I heard the report of Thee, and was afraid; glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^6571]

*Troparion 1:* The strength of my soul hath failed through poverty, O Mother of God, and grievous darkening hath fallen upon me, having its onset from transgressions; look down, as the light-bearing cloud of God, and enlighten me, I pray thee.[^6572]

*Troparion 2:* Turn the tempest of mine evils and the confusion of transgressions into the calm of salvation, since thou art a haven, O All-Immaculate One; roaring like lions they seek to devour me: deliver me, I pray thee, from their destruction.[^6573]

*Glory:* In night and in day, upon earth and at sea, and in every place, since thou art steadfast salvation, O Mother of God, an unconquerable protection, save me; for after God, we Christians truly place our hope in thee.[^6574]

*Both now... Theotokion:* From great and diverse transgressions and afflictions thou ever savest me; therefore, as having borne the Lord, O Virgin, I pray thee, and unto thee do I flee, the unconquerable help of the afflicted: bring me out of perils by thy supplications.[^6575]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Christ, Who didst cause the light to shine upon the world, enlighten my heart that cries unto Thee from the night, and save me.[^6576]

*Troparion 1:* As the shelter of salvation we glorify thee, O all-immaculate Mother of the Word, not fearing the plottings of men.[^6577]

*Troparion 2:* Having thee as an insurmountable wall, we are delivered from temptations and from the uprising of sorrows, O All-Pure One.[^6578]

*Glory:* Deliver me from the tongue of evil men, O Pure One; for like a razor hath it been sharpened, bitterly seeking to destroy my soul.[^6579]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Diligently do I fall down before thee, praying: as the Mother of my Creator, set me free from the perils that hold me fast.[^6580]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Devoured by the sea-monster of sin, I cry unto Thee, O Christ: As the prophet, set me free from corruption.[^6581]

*Troparion 1:* Having received the taste of bitterness, I have become estranged from divine sweetness, O All-Pure One! Therefore I cry unto thee: Grant me help.[^6582]

*Troparion 2:* The darkening of passions hath made me a slave of corruption; therefore, O Lady who didst bear the Light, set me free.[^6583]

*Glory:* In harmony and in faith do I confess thee, O All-Pure One, and I will sacrifice unto thee a sacrifice, having been delivered through thee from sorrow.[^6584]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The mouths of unrighteousness have opened against me, O Lady; therefore, as an intercessor, deliver me speedily from them.[^6585]

*Then, Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory... both now...*[^6586]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*
Unashamed protectress of Christians, unwavering mediatress with the Creator, despise not the voices of sinners in prayer, but in thy goodness anticipate us for our help who faithfully cry unto thee: hasten unto prayer and speed unto supplication, ever interceding, O Mother of God, for those who honor thee.[^6587]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The angel made the furnace a source of dew for the holy children, but the command of God burning up the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6588]

*Troparion 1:* Temptations have now surrounded my soul like fat bullocks; O most holy Virgin, do thou thyself set me free from them.[^6589]

*Troparion 2:* Thou diligently protectest those who are in peril and in sorrow, ever granting joy, O Mother of God.[^6590]

*Glory:* Vanquished entirely by despair of deeds and sorrow, encompass me with a shield, O Virgin, as the indestructible protection of all.[^6591]

*Both now... Theotokion:* By thine intercession we are delivered from perils and sorrows, and we find great and inexhaustible riches.[^6592]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Out of the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy children, and the sacrifice of the righteous man with the water Thou didst consume with fire; for Thou doest all things, O Christ, merely by willing them; Thee do we highly exalt unto all ages.[^6593]

*Troparion 1:* I am consumed by the malicious afflictions of men, and I pray thee, O Mother of God: Deliver me from their vain counsel.[^6594]

*Troparion 2:* I have been overcome in sorrows by my temptations, and I pray thee, O Lady: Save me unharmed from them.[^6595]

*Glory:* Save me, O Pure One, from deceitful man, and from the tongue and lips, and tyranny, and every distress.[^6596]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Being deceived in character and without excuse, I cry unto the Mother of God: Deliver me from all evils.[^6597]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* O Virgin who didst receive from the angel the greeting "Rejoice," and didst bear thine own Creator, save those who magnify thee.[^6598]

*Troparion 1:* Be merciful unto me, O Mother of God, in the sorrows of life, and save from perils him that now flees unto thee.[^6599]

*Troparion 2:* In truth, alone upon earth and at sea, thou hast been shown forth, O Pure One, an indestructible shelter unto those who flee unto thee with unhesitating soul.[^6600]

*Glory:* Unto me who have mindlessly enslaved myself unto every grievous fall, grant freedom through thy supplications, O All-Praised One.[^6601]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Having gained thee as a steadfast hope and protection, O Pure One, magnifying thee as the Mother of God, I conclude the hymn with faith.[^6602]

*Then, It is truly meet; Trisagion, and after Our Father: Hypakoe, and the rest as usual, and the dismissal.*[^6603]

*On Sunday Morning at the Midnight Office.*[^6604]
*Canon to the Most Holy and Life-Originating Trinity. (Whose acrostic is: "A sixth hymn I offer unto Thee, O Divinity"). The work of Metrophanes.*[^6605]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having walked on foot upon the deep with dryshod steps as upon dry land, Israel, beholding Pharaoh the pursuer drowned, cried: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^6606]
*Refrain: Most Holy Trinity, our God, glory to Thee.*[^6607]
*Troparion 1:* Three hypostases we hymn originating in God, the unchangeable form of a single nature, the good Lover of mankind, Who grants unto us the cleansing of transgressions.[^6608]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who art transcendent in essence, One, and thrice-radiant in Thy characters, O Lord, existing in one Godhead: give us understanding, and vouchsafe us Thy divine radiance.[^6609]

*Glory:* Adorning as a bride the Church from the nations, Paul taught us to worship Thee, the one God in three hypostases, from Whom and through Whom and in Whom all things were made.[^6610]

*Both now... Theotokion:* From thy womb came forth the spiritual Sun, O Mother of God, and shone upon us with the rays of the thrice-radiant Godhead; Whom hymning, with piety we call thee blessed.[^6611]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^6612]

*Troparion 1:* Having adorned the heavenly ranks, O thrice-radiant God, Thou hast ordained that they should sing unto Thee with thrice-holy voices; with them receive also us who hymn Thy goodness.[^6613]

*Troparion 2:* Hymning the one immutable, threefold, harmonious, solitary Principle of Divinity, we pray Thee fervently: send down now unto us the forgiveness of many sins.[^6614]

*Glory:* O Mind, unoriginate Father, co-essential Word of God, and divine Spirit, good and right: preserve those who faithfully hymn Thy dominion, as Thou art compassionate.[^6615]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The pasture of corruption among men did my God consume, becoming man in essence within thy womb, O Pure One; and alone He set free our forebears from the former condemnation.[^6616]

*Then, Lord, have mercy, thrice.*[^6617]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Fulfilling the dispensation for our sake" (Є҆́же ѡ҆ на́съ соверши́въ):*
O Master God, look down from heaven and behold our lowliness, as Thou art compassionate, and have mercy, O all-good Lover of mankind! For from nowhere do we hope to receive forgiveness of the evil things wherein we have sinned. Therefore be with us, and none can be against us.[^6618]
*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
O pure Lady, look down, O Mother of God; behold the pains of our wounds and have mercy, O All-Pure One, and heal the burning of conscience, bedewing it with thy mercy, and crying unto thy servants: I am with you, and none can be against you.[^6619]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church sings in godly manner, crying out from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^6620]

*Troparion 1:* O Thou Who liftest up the mind, O thrice-radiant Unity: speedily elevate the soul and heart of Thy chanters, and vouchsafe them Thy radiance and brightness.[^6621]

*Troparion 2:* Transform and reshape me from all malice unto virtue, O single, uncircumscribed, and immutable Trinity, and enlighten me with Thy rays.[^6622]

*Glory:* Having devised them beforehand, Thou didst wisely assemble the ministering ranks of angels of Thy goodness, O Unity in three hypostases: with them receive my praise.[^6623]

*Both now... Theotokion:* He Who by nature is uncreated God, co-eternal, taking upon Himself created human nature, reshaped it in thy holy womb, O Mother of God, Ever-Virgin.[^6624]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, illumine in love the souls of those who awake early unto Thee, I pray: that they may know Thee, O Word of God, as the true God, calling them back from the darkness of sin.[^6625]

*Troparion 1:* Meditating upon the divine Nature, providential and saving of all things, O Master, thrice-radiant and yet one, unto Thee do we awake early, asking forgiveness of transgressions.[^6626]

*Troparion 2:* O unoriginate Father and God, and co-eternal Son, and Holy Spirit: establish Thy chanters, O Trinity of solitary rule, and deliver them from every affliction and sorrow.[^6627]

*Glory:* Guide me by these godlike splendors, and ever directing me unto the pleasing of Thy Godhead in three hypostases, O Sun of glory, make me a partaker of the divine kingdom.[^6628]

*Both now... Theotokion:* O Word of God Who upholdest and preservest all things by Thine almighty hand, immutable One: preserve and keep those who glorify Thee, through the prayers of the Mother of God who bore Thee.[^6629]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing to Thy calm haven I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Greatly-Merciful One.[^6630]

*Troparion 1:* Wisdom and understanding, O thrice-radiant Principle of Divinity, grant unto Thy chanters, and with the beauties of Thy light-creating goodness vouchsafe all to be illumined. Twice.[^6631]

*Glory:* O Light indivisible in essence, thrice-radiant, almighty, unapproachable: enlighten the hearts of those who faithfully praise Thy dominion, and wing them toward divine love.[^6632]

*Both now... Theotokion:* In thee the Sustainer and Lord of all manifestly took up His abode, O Ever-Virgin, and taught men to worship the single, thrice-radiant form of the Godhead.[^6633]

*Lord, have mercy, thrice.*[^6634]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Fulfilling the dispensation for our sake" (Є҆́же ѡ҆ на́съ):*
O Father and Son with the Holy Spirit, look down upon us who with faith worship Thee, and who glorify Thy dominion, O Compassionate One, dust mingling with the fiery hosts; for we know none other besides Thee; and cry aloud unto those who hymn Thee: I am with you, and none can be against you.[^6635]
*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Look down upon us, O all-praised Mother of God; cause enlightenment to dawn upon darkened hearts, and illumine thy flock, O All-Pure One! For whatsoever thou wilt, thou canst do, as being the Mother of thy Creator; and cry aloud unto those who pray to thee: I am with you, and none can be against you.[^6636]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The angel made the furnace a source of dew for the holy children, but the command of God burning up the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6637]

*Troparion 1:* Establish a steadfast mind in me, O Unity thrice-radiant in Thy characters, that I may keep and observe Thy divine commandments, and ever sing unto Thee with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6638]

*Troparion 2:* As hymned in the sameness of nature, O ineffable God, yet bearing the number of a Trinity in Persons: preserve us all from diverse temptations and distresses.[^6639]

*Glory:* Co-essential and co-eternal do we glorify Thee, the one God in essence, O Trinity, unconfused in hypostatic properties, simply presenting what is distinct in an unchangeable form manifestly.[^6640]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The transcendent God, O All-Pure One, manifestly took from thy pure womb our mixture out of love for mankind, and taught all to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6641]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Out of the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy children, and the sacrifice of the righteous man with the water Thou didst consume with fire; for Thou doest all things, O Christ, merely by willing them; Thee do we highly exalt unto all ages.[^6642]

*Troparion 1:* Cleansing of transgressions speedily grant unto me, and deliverance from manifold passions, O harmonious Trinity, Unity in three hypostases! That I may glorify Thee unto all ages.[^6643]

*Troparion 2:* Confirmed as the Lover of mercy, as Thou art a merciful God, have mercy upon all, O thrice-radiant Unity, and all-good Trinity, who praise Thy majesty.[^6644]

*Glory:* From the Light of the co-eternal Father, the Word born as Light co-eternal, together with the Spirit Who proceeds as Light, with faith we glorify and highly exalt unto all ages.[^6645]

*Both now... Theotokion:* A Physician for men hast thou borne, O All-Pure One: Christ the Lord, the all-powerful Word, Who heals the ancestral wounds of all that highly exalt Him unto the ages.[^6646]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure One, the incarnate Word was shown forth unto men; Whom magnifying together with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^6647]

*Troparion 1:* The ranks of the Cherubim, O Master, unable to gaze upon the beauty of Thy glory, covering themselves with their wings, unceasingly cry the thrice-holy hymn, glorifying the dominion of Thy single, thrice-radiant Principle of Divinity.[^6648]

*Troparion 2:* Grant Thy splendors into the hearts of Thy servants, O unsetting Sun; and enlighten our souls, and deliver us from many transgressions, O only All-Merciful One in three hypostases; and vouchsafe us Thy incorruptible life.[^6649]

*Glory:* O Deity existing as Light equally honored, thrice-radiant, and light-creating: illumine those who hymn Thee with faith; and deliver us from dark malice, and vouchsafe us Thy most radiant habitations, as Thou art all-good.[^6650]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Most wisely did thy Son create man in the beginning, O Virgin, and through thee, O All-Praised One, He renewed him when corrupted; and with the unwaning rays of divine light He filled all who glorify thee in faith as the true Mother of God.[^6651]

*After this, the refrains of Gregory the Sinaite, and the rest of the Midnight Office.*[^6652]

*On Sunday at Matins.*[^6653]
*After the Six Psalms: "God is the Lord," in Tone 6. After this, the Resurrection Troparion: "The angelic powers were upon Thy tomb," twice. Glory: troparion of the Saint; Both now: Theotokion in the Tone of the troparion of the Saint. Then the customary reading of the Psalter.*[^6654]

*After the 1st Kathisma, Resurrection Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*
While the tomb was open and hades was weeping, Mary cried aloud unto the hidden Apostles: Come forth, ye laborers of the vineyard, proclaim the word of the Resurrection: The Lord is risen, granting unto the world great mercy.[^6655]
*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy paupers unto the end.*[^6656]
Life lay in the tomb, and a seal was placed upon the stone; as a sleeping king the soldiers guarded Christ; and having invisibly smitten His enemies, the Lord is risen.[^6657]
*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
O Virgin Mother of God, entreat thy Son, Christ our God, Who was willingly nailed upon the Cross and is risen from the dead, that our souls may be saved.[^6658]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Resurrection Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*
O Lord, Mary Magdalene stood before Thy tomb, and she wept, crying aloud, and supposing Thee to be the gardener, she said: Where hast Thou hidden the eternal Life? Where hast Thou laid Him Who sits upon the Cherubic throne? For those who guarded Him have become as dead men from fear. Either give me my Lord, or cry out with me: O Thou Who wast among the dead, and hast raised up the dead, glory to Thee.[^6659]
*Verse: I will praise Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will show forth all Thy marvelous works.*[^6660]
Jonah prefigured Thy tomb, and Symeon declares Thy divine rising, O immortal Lord: for Thou didst descend as a dead man into the tomb, Who didst destroy the gates of hades; and Thou didst rise without corruption as Master, for the salvation of the world, O Christ our God, Who didst enlighten those in darkness.[^6661]
*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Gideon prefigured the conception, and David declares thy childbirth, O Mother of God: for as dew upon the fleece did the Word descend into thy womb, and without seed didst thou bud forth, O holy earth, for the salvation of the world: Christ our God, O Full of Grace.[^6662]

*After the Evlogetaria, Hypakoe, Tone 6:*
By Thy voluntary and life-creating death, O Christ, having shattered the gates of hades as God, Thou didst open unto us the ancient Paradise; and having risen from the dead, Thou didst deliver our life from corruption.[^6663]

*Hymns of Ascent, Tone 6:*
*Antiphon 1:*
Unto heaven do I lift up mine eyes, unto Thee, O Word: have compassion upon me, that I may live unto Thee.[^6664]
Have mercy upon us who are brought to naught, fashioning Thy well-fitted vessels, O Word.[^6665]
*Glory:* Unto the Holy Spirit belongs every all-saving cause: if upon whomsoever He breathes according to worthiness, quickly He takes him from earthly things, wings him, makes him grow, and sets him on high.[^6666]
*Both now... the same.*[^6667]

*Antiphon 2:*
If the Lord had not been in us, none of us could have withstood the enemy's warfare; for they that conquer are exalted from hence.[^6668]
Let not my soul be caught in their teeth as a bird, O Word: woe is me! How shall I escape from the enemy, being a lover of sin?[^6669]
*Glory:* Through the Holy Spirit is deification unto all, good pleasure, understanding, peace, and blessing; for He is of equal operation with the Father and the Word.[^6670]
*Both now... the same.*[^6671]

*Antiphon 3:*
They that hope in the Lord are fearful unto enemies, and marvelous unto all; for they look on high.[^6672]
Unto iniquities the portion of the righteous puts not forth its hands, having Thee as a helper, O Savior.[^6673]
*Glory:* The dominion of the Holy Spirit is over all: Whom the hosts on high worship, together with every breath below.[^6674]
*Both now... the same.*[^6675]

*Prokeimenon, Tone 6:*
O Lord, stir up Thy might, and come to save us.[^6676]
*Verse: Give ear, O Shepherd of Israel, Thou that leadest Joseph like a sheep.*[^6677]
*Let every breath praise the Lord.*[^6678]
*Verse: Praise God in His saints.*[^6679]
*The Resurrection Gospel. "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ"; Psalm 50, and the rest in order.*[^6680]

*Resurrection Canon.*
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having walked on foot upon the deep with dryshod steps as upon dry land, Israel, beholding Pharaoh the pursuer drowned, cried: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^6681]
*Refrain: Glory to Thy holy Resurrection, O Lord.*[^6682]
*Troparion 1:* With outstretched hands upon the Cross, O good Jesus, Thou didst fulfill the Father's good pleasure in all things: therefore a song of victory do we all sing unto Thee.[^6683]
*Troparion 2:* With fear before Thee as a handmaid, death when commanded drew near unto the Master of life, Who through it grants unto us unending life and resurrection.[^6684]
*Theotokion:* Having received thine own Creator, as He Himself willed, incarnate beyond comprehension from thy seedless womb, O Pure One, thou hast truly been shown forth the Lady of creation.[^6685]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* He Who with a wave of the sea hid the pursuing tyrant of old, the children of the saved hid beneath the earth; but let us, as the maidens, sing unto the Lord: For He hath been greatly glorified.[^6686]
*Troparion 1:* With fear the gates of death open unto Thee, and the eternal bars are shattered: for by Thy venerable descent, the dead of old rose again, thankfully singing Thy Resurrection, O Christ.[^6687]
*Troparion 2:* In the lowest pit wast Thou laid, O Christ, having become dead; and Thou didst put to death the slayer, and the prisoners who were in hades didst Thou set free, who glorify Thy venerable Resurrection, O Master.[^6688]
*Theotokion:* Rejoice, light-bearing tabernacle and candlestick, church, and table, and holy mountain! Rejoice, palace of glory, and throne of our God! Rejoice, insurmountable wall, unto us who hymn thee.[^6689]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*(Whose acrostic is: "O Mother of God, grant me unstinted grace").*[^6690]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos: Having walked on foot upon the deep:*[^6691]
*Troparion 1:* Having partaken of the food of disobedience from the tree, Eve brought in the curse; but this hast thou dissolved, O Pure One, having borne Christ, the Firstfruit of blessing.[^6692]
*Troparion 2:* O thou who didst bear Christ, the Pearl from divine lightning: the gloom of my passions, and of transgressions...*[^6693]

...the disturbance of transgressions do thou drive away, O Pure One, by the light of thy radiance.[^6694]

*Troparion 3:* Jacob secretly foresaw with spiritual eyes God, the Expectation of the nations, becoming incarnate from thee, Who hath delivered us through thy mediation.[^6695]

*Troparion 4:* When rulers from the tribe of Judah had failed, O Most Pure One, Thy Son and God, having come as Leader, hath now truly reigned over the ends of the earth.[^6696]

*Katavasia: I shall open my mouth:*[^6697]

*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^6698]

*Troparion 1:* Beholding God crucified in the flesh, creation melted away with fear; but by the sustaining hand of Him Who was crucified for our sake, it was firmly held together.[^6699]

*Troparion 2:* By death is wretched death overthrown and lies breathless; for unable to endure the assault of divine life, the strong one is put to death, and resurrection is granted unto all.[^6700]

*Theotokion:* The wonder of thy divine childbirth, O Pure One, surpasses every order of nature; for supernaturally didst thou conceive God in thy womb, and having given birth, thou remainest Ever-Virgin.[^6701]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos:* Beholding Thee hanging upon Calvary, Who didst suspend the whole earth upon the waters without restraint, creation quaked with great terror, crying aloud: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord.[^6702]

*Troparion 1:* Confessing Thee Who didst rise on the third day and appeared unto the Myrrh-bearing women, O Savior, and said unto them: "Rejoice!" we say: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord, Lover of mankind.[^6703]

*Troparion 2:* Glory to Thy rising, O our Savior, for Thou hast delivered us from the corruption of hades and from death, as almighty; and singing, we say: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord, Lover of mankind.[^6704]

*Theotokion:* We piously glorify Thee, the Word incarnate of the holy Maiden of God, Who hast saved the fallen first-created man from deception: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord, Lover of mankind.[^6705]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos: There is none holy as Thou, O Lord:*[^6706]

*Troparion 1:* Having ineffably taken from thy womb my corruptible and mortal flesh, O all-pure Mother of God...

...the Good One, having made it incorruptible, bound it unto Himself eternally.[^6707]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding God becoming incarnate from thee, O Virgin, the angelic choirs were struck with dread; and as the Mother of God, with unceasing hymns do they honor thee.[^6708]

*Troparion 3:* The Prophet Daniel was amazed, beholding thee as a spiritual mountain from which a Stone was cut out without hands, which mightily shattered the shrines of demons, O Mother of God.[^6709]

*Troparion 4:* Human word, nor tongue, O Virgin, can worthily praise thee; for from thee without seed Christ the Giver of life was well pleased to become incarnate, O Most Pure One.[^6710]

*Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church piously sings, crying out with a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^6711]

*Troparion 1:* The tree of true life blossomed forth, O Christ, for the Cross was set up; and being watered with blood and water from Thine incorruptible side, it budded forth life unto us.[^6712]

*Troparion 2:* No longer does the serpent falsely suggest deification unto me; for Christ, the Deifier of human nature, hath now unhindered opened the path of life unto me.[^6713]

*Theotokion:* Truly unsearchable and incomprehensible unto those on earth and in heaven, O Ever-Virgin Mother of God, are the mysteries of thy divinely fitting childbirth.[^6714]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Foreseeing Thy divine emptying upon the Cross, Habakkuk cried out in terror: Thou hast severed the dominion of the mighty, O Good One, partaking with those in hades, as almighty.[^6715]

*Troparion 1:* He Who divinely abides with the Father on high was bodily nailed to the tree for our sake, annulling the curse that occurred in Paradise, as almighty.[^6716]

*Troparion 2:* The all-pure flesh of the Deliverer lying in the tomb suffered no corruption; but as almighty, Thou didst shatter the bars of hades, and didst rise in glory on the third day, O Christ.[^6717]

*Theotokion:* Having risen from the dead, our Savior called out unto the Myrrh-bearing women: "Rejoice!" and cried unto them: "Go forth now, proclaim the good tidings unto My Apostles!"[^6718]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos: Christ is my power, God and Lord:*[^6719]

*Troparion 1:* We who have been saved through thee hymn thee, O all-immaculate, Most Pure One, and piously singing we cry aloud: Blessed art thou, O Ever-Virgin, who hast given birth unto God.[^6720]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast given birth unto the unwaning Light shining in the flesh for those in the darkness of this life, O all-blessed Virgin, and unto them that hymn thee hast thou poured forth joy, O Ever-Virgin.[^6721]

*Troparion 3:* Grace blossomed forth, the Law ceased through thee, O all-hymned one; for thou, O Pure One, hast given birth unto the Lord Who grants us remission, O Ever-Virgin.[^6722]

*Troparion 4:* The tasting of the tree proved me dead; but the Tree of Life shown forth from thee, O Most Pure One, hath resurrected me and made me an heir of the delight of Paradise.[^6723]

*Tone 6. Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, illuminate, I pray Thee, the souls of those who dawn early unto Thee with love, that they may know Thee, O Word of God, the true God, Who callest them from the gloom of sin.[^6724]

*Troparion 1:* The Cherubim give way before me now, and the flaming sword turns its back unto me, O Master, having beheld Thee, O Word of God, the true God, Who didst make a way into Paradise for the thief.[^6725]

*Troparion 2:* No longer do I fear the return into the earth, O Master Christ; for by Thy resurrection Thou hast raised me up from the earth, forgotten though I was, unto the height of incorruption, by reason of Thine abundant compassions.[^6726]

*Theotokion:* Save those who from the soul confess thee to be the Mother of God, O good Lady of the world; for we have thee as an invincible protection, the true Birthgiver of God.[^6727]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Having beheld the unwaning light of Thy divine epiphany which mercifully came unto us, O Christ, Isaiah dawning early from the night cried aloud: The dead shall arise, and they that are in the graves shall be raised up, and all earthborn shall rejoice.[^6728]

*Troparion 1:* For compassion's sake, O Good One, taking a form like unto us, Thou wast voluntarily nailed upon the Cross for my sake, who formerly cast away Thy holy commandment for the sake of food; and Thou diest in a manner fitting for man, bestowing life upon me.[^6729]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding Thee upon the throne as God, and yet numbered among the dead as a dead man, wretched hades vomited forth those whom it formerly held; and by Thy three-day might...

...Thou didst raise up fallen nature together with Thyself, and Thou Thyself hast enlightened the whole world, O Christ, as merciful.[^6730]

*Theotokion:* Thou sufferedst no corruption of the womb, having endured virginal childbirth; but again, even as before, hast thou remained, O Mother of God, unwedded Mother. Therefore pray earnestly for thy flock, to deliver from temptations and distresses those who glorify thee.[^6731]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 5. Heirmos: With Thy divine light, O Good One:*[^6732]

*Troparion 1:* Shining brightly in purity, thou wast the divine abode of the Master, O all-hymned one; for thou alone hast been shown forth as the Mother of God, having carried Him in thine arms as an infant.[^6733]

*Troparion 2:* Bearing the spiritual beauty of thine all-fair soul, thou wast the bride of God, sealed with virginity, O Pure One, and enlightening the world with the light of purity.[^6734]

*Troparion 3:* Let the congregation of the wicked wail, who do not openly proclaim thee pure Mother of God; for thou hast been shown forth unto us as the gate of God's light, dispelling the gloom of transgressions.[^6735]

*Tone 6. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, having fled to Thy calm haven, I cry unto Thee: Bring up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One.[^6736]

*Troparion 1:* Being crucified, O Master, by nails Thou didst blot out the curse that was against us; and being pierced in the side with a spear, having torn up the handwriting against Adam, Thou didst set the world free.[^6737]

*Troparion 2:* Adam was brought down, supplanted by deceit into the abyss of hades; but Thou Who art by nature God and merciful didst descend to seek him out, and bearing him upon Thy shoulders, didst raise him up with Thyself.[^6738]

*Theotokion:* O Most Pure Lady, who hast borne the Lord, the Helmsman of mankind: quell the unstable and cruel tumult of my passions, and grant tranquility unto my heart.[^6739]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah was swallowed, yet not held in the entrails of the sea monster; for bearing an image of Thee Who sufferedst and wast given over to burial, coming forth from the beast as from a bridal chamber, he cried out unto the watch: Ye that observe vain and false things have forsaken this mercy.[^6740]

*Troparion 1:* How didst Thou endure wounds, and insults, and buffeting, O Word? For Thou...

...voluntarily as man in the flesh didst endure suffering; yet being God, Thou didst remain impassible, crucified upon the tree between two thieves; and Thou hast saved our nature from corruption, as almighty.[^6741]

*Troparion 2:* Of Thine own will wast Thou laid in the tomb for my sake, O Savior, as a dead man; and Thou didst shine forth light upon those held in darkness and the shadow of death; and having shone forth on the third day and met the Myrrh-bearing women, Thou didst cry unto them thus: Truly rejoice, and henceforth tell My Apostles![^6742]

*Theotokion:* O Virgin, favored Mother, Bride of God! having without knowing man received into thy womb Him Who before all ages was born of the unoriginate Father, thou hast given birth unto Him incarnate; Whom do thou beseech for us all, to send down the multitude of His compassions upon us.[^6743]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 6. Heirmos: Beholding the sea of life surging:*[^6744]

*Troparion 1:* Moses, great among the prophets, prefigured thee as the ark, and the table, and the candlestick, and the jar: figuratively signifying the incarnation of the Most High from thee, O Mother-Virgin.[^6745]

*Troparion 2:* Death is put to death, and corruption is abolished, which dashed itself against the Fruit of thy womb, O Lady, from the condemnation of Adam; for thou hast given birth unto Life, Which delivers from corruption them that hymn thee.[^6746]

*Troparion 3:* The Law grew weak, and the shadow passed away, when the grace beyond mind and understanding was revealed unto me in the childbirth from thee, O Virgin, of God and the Savior, O all-hymned one.[^6747]

*Kontakion, Tone 6:* With His life-originating hand, having resurrected all the dead from the murky vales, Christ God, the Giver of life, granted resurrection unto the human race; for He is the Savior of all, the resurrection, and life, and God of all.[^6748]

*Ikos:* Thy Cross and burial do we faithful hymn, O Giver of life, and we worship Thee, for Thou hast bound hades, O Immortal One, as God almighty; and Thou didst raise up the dead together with Thyself, and didst shatter the gates of death, and cast down the dominion of hades as God. Therefore we earthborn glorify Thee with love, Who didst rise, and cast down the all-destructive dominion of the enemy, and...

...raised up all that have believed in Thee, and delivered the world from the serpents' arrows and from the deception of the enemy, as God of all.[^6749]

*Tone 6. Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Angel made the furnace pour forth dew for the holy children, but the command of God burning the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6750]

*Troparion 1:* Mourning at Thy Passion, the sun clothed itself in darkness, and in daytime across all the earth the light was darkened, O Master, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6751]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy descent into the nethermost parts, O Christ, they were clothed in light; and the forefather, appearing full of gladness, leaped for joy, dancing and crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6752]

*Triadikon:* Singing, let us cry aloud, O faithful, unto the Trinity ruling over all, tri-hypostatic by nature, the unsearchable goodness and abyss of light: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6753]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 7. Heirmos:* Ineffable wonder! He Who delivered the holy children from the flame in the furnace is laid in the tomb as dead and breathless, for the salvation of us who sing: O God our Deliverer, blessed art Thou.[^6754]

*Troparion 1:* The impious children of the lawless crucified Thee upon Calvary, O Savior, Who didst break the brazen gates and bars, for the salvation of us who sing: O God our Deliverer, blessed art Thou.[^6755]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast destroyed the dominion of death, and having risen, grantest resurrection unto those who truly glorify Thee as Lord, and orthodoxly cry unto Thee with faith: O God our Deliverer, blessed art Thou.[^6756]

*Theotokion:* One of the Trinity hast thou borne, O Most Pure One, Who for mercy's sake appeared upon earth in two natures; Whom do thou unceasingly beseech to save our souls.[^6757]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 7. Heirmos: The Angel made the furnace pour forth dew:*[^6758]

*Troparion 1:* The furnace burned not the three youths who prefigured thy childbirth; for the divine Fire, without burning thee, dwelt within thee and taught all to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6759]

*Troparion 2:* The ends of the earth call thee blessed, O all-pure Mother, even as thou didst foretell, being enlightened by thy light-bearing rays, and by grace...

...singing, they cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6760]

*Troparion 3:* The most deceitful serpent indeed sank destructive fangs into me; but Thy Son Himself shattered them, O Mother of God, and gave me strength to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6761]

*Troparion 4:* Thou alone art the propitiation of our nature, O divinely blessed one; for bearing in thine arms God Who sits upon the Cherubic shoulders, thou criest aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6762]

*Tone 6. Ode 8. Heirmos:* From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and with water didst burn up the sacrifice of the righteous man; for Thou dost all things, O Christ, merely by willing them: Thee do we highly exalt unto all ages.[^6763]

*Troparion 1:* Envy hath now made the Jewish people, who of old slew the prophets, to become slayers of God, having lifted Thee upon the Cross, O Word of God: Whom we highly exalt unto all ages.[^6764]

*Troparion 2:* Without leaving the celestial sphere, and having descended into hades, Thou didst raise up the whole man who lay in the dunghill, O Christ, who highly exalts Thee unto all ages.[^6765]

*Triadikon:* The one nature of the Trinity, of innumerable might, surpassing every word and nature and thought, we ever orthodoxly proclaim, and highly exalt unto ages.[^6766]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Be astounded, O heaven, with fear, and let the foundations of the earth be shaken: for lo, He Who lives in the highest is numbered among the dead, and is wondrously received into a small tomb. Bless Him, ye children; praise Him, ye priests; highly exalt Him, ye peoples, unto all ages.[^6767]

*Troparion 1:* Every ear was struck with awe, how the Most High voluntarily came upon the earth to destroy the power of hades by the Cross and burial, and to raise all to cry: Bless Him, ye children; praise Him, ye priests; highly exalt Him, ye peoples, unto all ages.[^6768]

*Troparion 2:* O Thine ineffable love for mankind, O Christ, and unspeakable good things! For seeing me perishing in the prison of hades, Thou didst endure suffering and delivered me. Therefore we bless Thee, Master of all, and highly exalt Thee unto all ages.[^6769]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, Gate of God! Rejoice, rampart of the faithful! Rejoice, unwedded Mary! Rejoice, all-pure Mother of God! Rejoice, who alone didst bear God for earthborn mortals, unto Whom we cry: Children, bless...

...bless; ye priests, hymn; ye peoples, highly exalt Him unto the ages.[^6770]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 8. Heirmos: From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew:*[^6771]

*Troparion 1:* Having enlightened thee with the radiance of the Spirit in a vesture inwrought with gold as a queen, Thy Son set thee at His right hand, O Most Pure One: Whom we highly exalt unto all ages.[^6772]

*Troparion 2:* He Who by His sole will established the world takes flesh from thy most pure womb, willing to build it anew on high: Whom we highly exalt unto all ages.[^6773]

*Troparion 3:* By the union of the Word with me, man, thou wast the divine habitation, O Most Pure One, having manifestly shone forth with the brightness of virginity: therefore we hymn thee unto all ages.[^6774]

*Troparion 4:* The gold-shining candlestick prefigured thee, who didst ineffably receive the unapproachable Light, Which by Its understanding enlightens all things: therefore we hymn thee, O Pure One, unto the ages.[^6775]

*My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^6776]

*Tone 6. Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure One, was the Word revealed incarnate unto men: Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^6777]

*Troparion 1:* In no wise partaking of passions didst Thou remain, O Word of God, though partaking of passions in the flesh; but Thou loosest man from passions, having become a passion unto passions, O our Savior: for Thou alone art impassible and almighty.[^6778]

*Troparion 2:* Having received mortal dissolution, Thou didst preserve Thy body without tasting of corruption; nor was Thine all-holy and divine soul left in hades, O Master; but having risen as from sleep, Thou didst raise us up with Thyself.[^6779]

*Triadikon:* God the Father, and the co-unoriginate Son do all we men glorify with pure lips, and the ineffable and most glorious power of the all-holy Spirit do we revere: for Thou art the one almighty and indivisible Trinity.[^6780]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 6. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Lament Me not, O Mother, beholding in the tomb the Son Whom thou didst conceive in the womb without seed; for I shall arise and be glorified, and as God I shall unceasingly exalt in glory them that magnify thee with faith and love.[^6781]

*Troparion 1:* Though Thou didst descend into the tomb as dead, O Giver of life, yet Thou didst destroy the power of hades, O Christ, raising up together the dead whom it had formerly swallowed; and...

...granted resurrection unto all who magnify Thee with faith and love, as God.[^6782]

*Troparion 2:* Let creation robe itself in white, let all earthborn mortals rejoice, for the enemy hades hath been despoiled! Christ is risen, and the first-created man is risen together in glory, delivered from corruption! the Angel cries unto the Myrrh-bearing women.[^6783]

*Troparion 3:* Let creation rejoice, and let it blossom as a lily, for Christ is risen from the dead as God! Where now, O death, is thy sting? let us cry: Where, O hades, is thy victory? He Who exalted our horn hath cast thee down into the earth, as He is compassionate.[^6784]

*Theotokion:* Creation rejoices together in thy childbirth, O Virgin; for Bethlehem hath opened Eden unto us; and enjoying in faith the Tree of this Life, we earnestly cry aloud: Fulfill our supplications, O Lady.[^6785]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 9. Heirmos: It is impossible for men to see God:*[^6786]

*Troparion 1:* A Star shining with the beams of the Godhead out of Jacob hath shone forth upon those held fast in gloom; for through thee, O All-Pure One, Christ God the Word was made incarnate: by Whom being enlightened, with the heavenly hosts we call thee blessed.[^6787]

*Troparion 2:* Strengthened by thy might and grace, from my heart have I earnestly offered a song unto thee; but receive it, O pure Virgin, bestowing in return thy most radiant grace from Thine incorruptible treasures, O divinely blessed one.[^6788]

*Troparion 3:* Thou hast manifestly been shown forth as the loom of the Godhead, wherein the Word wove the vesture of the body, deifying my form, O Virgin; having put on which, He saved all who with a pure mind magnify thee.[^6789]

*Troparion 4:* Resurrection hath now been granted unto the dead through thine ineffable and unspeakable childbirth, O all-pure Mother of God; for Life, having clothed Itself in flesh from thee, hath shone forth upon all and manifestly destroyed the somberness of death.[^6790]

*After the Katavasia, Little Litany. Then: Holy is the Lord our God: The Matins Exapostilarion.*[^6791]

*At the Praises, Resurrection Stichera, Tone 6:*[^6792]

*Sticheron 1:* Thy Cross, O Lord, is life and resurrection unto Thy people; and trusting therein, we praise Thee, our risen God: Have mercy upon us.[^6793]

*Sticheron 2:* Thy burial, O Master, opened Paradise unto the human race; and delivered from corruption, we praise Thee, our risen God: Have mercy upon us.[^6794]

*Sticheron 3:* With the Father and the Spirit let us praise Christ, Who is risen from the dead, and let us cry unto Him: Thou art our life and resurrection: Have mercy upon us.[^6795]

*Sticheron 4:* On the third day Thou didst rise from the tomb, O Christ, even as it is written, having raised up our forefather with Thyself: therefore the human race also glorifies Thee, and hymns Thy resurrection.[^6796]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian, in the same Tone:*[^6797]

*Sticheron 5:* O Lord, great and awesome is the mystery of Thy resurrection; for thus didst Thou come forth from the tomb as a bridegroom from his chamber, having destroyed death by death, that Thou mightest set Adam free. Therefore in the heavens the Angels dance, and on earth men glorify Thy tender mercy shown towards us, O Lover of mankind.[^6798]

*Sticheron 6:* O most lawless Judeans! where are the seals, and the pieces of silver which ye gave unto the soldiers? The Treasure was not stolen, but He is risen as mighty; but ye yourselves were put to shame, having rejected Christ, the Lord of glory, Who suffered and was buried, and rose from the dead: Him let us worship.[^6799]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor for ever.*[^6800]

*Sticheron 7:* While the tomb was sealed, how were ye despoiled, O Judeans, having set guards and laid signs? While the doors were shut, the King came forth. Either produce Him as dead, or worship Him as God, singing with us: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and to Thy resurrection.[^6801]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*[^6802]

*Sticheron 8:* Unto Thy life-receiving tomb the Myrrh-bearing women weeping arrived, O Lord, and carrying myrrh, they sought to anoint Thine all-pure Body; but they found a light-bearing Angel sitting upon the stone, and speaking unto them, and saying: Why do ye weep for Him Who poured forth life unto the world from His side? Why do ye seek the Immortal One in the tomb as dead? Run rather and declare unto His disciples His glorious...[^6803]

...resurrection, the joy of the whole world. Wherewith having also enlightened us, O Savior, grant cleansing and great mercy.

*Glory: The Morning Gospel Sticheron; and both now: Theotokion: "Most blessed art thou": Great Doxology, Troparion: "Having risen from the tomb": Litanies, and Dismissal.*[^6804]

*At the Liturgy:*
*The Beatitudes, Tone 6:*[^6805]

*Refrain:* Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom, and save me, as Thou alone art the Lover of mankind.[^6806]

*Troparion 1:* Adam having been deceived by a tree, by the Tree of the Cross Thou didst again save him, and the thief who cried: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom.[^6807]

*Troparion 2:* Let us sing of Christ our God, Who of His own will was crucified and buried, and rose in glory on the third day.[^6808]

*Troparion 3:* Having shattered the gates and bars of hades, O Giver of life, Thou didst raise up all those in hades who cry aloud: Glory to Thy resurrection![^6809]

*Troparion 4:* Having risen from the tomb, even as He foretold before the Passion, and having despoiled hades, let us all with one accord sing of Christ.[^6810]

*Troparion 5:* Having been shown forth as the light of the world, the Apostles drove away all the gloom of deceit by the divine rays of their teachings.[^6811]

*Glory... Triadikon:* We glorify the Father and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, saying: O Holy Trinity, save our souls.[^6812]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, O gate opened unto God, Who ineffably made His abode in thee, and came forth from thee, O Ever-Virgin.[^6813]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers

*On Sunday Evening:*[^6814]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera.*[^6815]
*(A composition of Joseph).*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "The Angelic Powers" (А҆́гг҃лскїѧ си́лы):*[^6816]

*Sticheron 1:* Grant me tears of repentance, O Savior, with a contrite mind, that I may wash away the defilement of my soul, which I have evilly fouled by doing unrighteous things at every hour; having to intercede for this with Thee...

...her that gave Thee birth, and the Angelic choirs, and Thine own love for mankind.[^6817]

*Sticheron 2:* Come, O soul, and repent of the many sins which thou hast committed in life; and pray unto the multitude of the heavenly hosts with sighing and tears, that time for repentance may be granted unto thee, lest like the barren fig tree thou be cast into the fire of Gehenna, O most wretched one.[^6818]

*Sticheron 3:* Not a single commandment of Thine have I kept upon earth; how then shall I appear before Thee seated upon the throne, to render an account and to undergo judgment for all that in knowledge and in ignorance I have done, O my Christ? Therefore I cry unto Thee: By the intercessions of Thy servants, save me the prodigal.[^6819]

*Then the Stichera from the Menaion for the Saint; but if there is no Menaion, these other Stichera to the holy heavenly Bodiless Angels:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having risen on the third day" (Тридне́внѡ воскре́слъ є҆сѝ):*[^6820]

*Sticheron 4:* The Angelic Powers, O Christ, standing before Thy throne, pray for the human race. By their prayers therefore grant peace unto all, and tame the audacity of the nations.[^6821]

*Sticheron 5:* All ye ranks of Angels, ever dancing in choir round about the throne of the King of all, preserve us who call upon you in faith, and deliver us from torments.[^6822]

*Sticheron 6:* God the Father Who is over all, and the consubstantial Word, and the Spirit, brought into being the hosts of the heavenly minds, bodiless and immaterial singers of His thrice-radiant glory.[^6823]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Having conceived in thy womb at the Archangel's voice, O Mother of God, the Word co-unoriginate with the Father and the Spirit, thou hast been shown forth higher than the Cherubim, the Seraphim, and the Thrones.[^6824]

*O Joyful Light: Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord, to keep us this evening:*[^6825]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 6:*[^6826]

*Sticheron 1:* At Thy dread coming, O Christ, let us not hear: I know you not; for in Thee, O Savior, have we placed our hope; though through our negligence we have not kept Thy commandments, yet spare our souls, we pray Thee.[^6827]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^6828]

*Sticheron 2:* Repentance have I not acquired, neither again tears; therefore I pray Thee, O Christ God, that before the end I may turn back, and that Thou grant me compunction, that I may be delivered from torment.[^6829]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are exceedingly...*

...filled with contempt. Our soul is filled exceedingly: a reproach to them that are at ease, and a contempt to the proud.*[^6830]

*Martyrikon:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, denied Thee not, neither did they depart from Thy commandments. By their prayers have mercy upon us.[^6831]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Let us the faithful sing in Archangelic manner of the heavenly bridal chamber, and the door truly sealed: Rejoice, for whose sake the Savior of all sprang up for us, Christ, the Giver of life and God. Cast down, O Lady, the tyrants, our godless enemies, by thy hand, O All-Pure One, the hope of Christians.[^6832]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion Prayers; after Our Father: Troparion, Litany, and Dismissal.*[^6833]

*On Sunday at Compline:*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Traversing the deep on foot as if on dry land, Israel, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh engulfed, cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^6834]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^6835]

*Troparion 1:* Drive away the cloud of passions from my mind, O Pure One, and make light to shine upon me, O All-Pure One who gavest birth to the Son, the Radiance of the Father, that I may sing of thee.[^6836]

*Troparion 2:* Like Gabriel unto the Virgin, come, ye faithful, let us also cry with soul's reverence: Truly adorned art thou, O Pure One, who gavest birth to joy: Rejoice, O All-Pure One![^6837]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Having borne in thy womb in the last days the Son Who was impassibly begotten of the Father before the ages, O All-Pure One, thou didst give birth to Him twofold, God and man.[^6838]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Thou alone without corruption didst give birth to God, and didst remain a Virgin after childbirth, even as thou wast before childbirth, O All-Pure One: therefore we the faithful cry unto thee: Rejoice![^6839]

*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy like unto Thee, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^6840]

*Troparion 1:* There is none blameless like unto thee, O all-pure Mother of God, who gavest birth to the Creator and Master of all things: therefore all we faithful cry aloud unto thee in thanksgiving: Rejoice![^6841]

*Troparion 2:* The fierce surge of passions troubles me, O Pure One, and with the bitternesses...

...of many fallings, and casts me truly into the depth of my evils; reach out thy hand unto me, O Pure One, and save me.[^6842]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Dispel the darkness of my transgressions, O Virgin, and illumine me with the light of dispassion, O Pure One who ineffably didst give birth to Life, the Sun unapproachable.[^6843]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Having cleansed me from transgressions and defilement by the fire of the fear of God, O Maiden, clothe me in the radiant garment of virtues, and unite me unto the choirs of the saints.[^6844]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, my God and Lord, the venerable Church godly sings, crying aloud from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^6845]

*Troparion 1:* Having given birth unto the Light, dispel the darkness of my many transgressions by thy beam, O all-immaculate Lady, who didst cause the Sun of Righteousness to shine forth upon the faithful.[^6846]

*Troparion 2:* Unto thee, O All-Pure One, do I now offer the hope of my infirmity, that I may not depart from thee without effect, but having obtained salvation, may sing: Rejoice![^6847]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Bestow thy rich compassions upon us, O All-Pure One, who flee with faith and love into thy temple, and deliver us from all temptations and afflictions.[^6848]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* The Word made His abode in thee, O All-Pure One, delivering the world from the irrationality of passions, O Lady, and making those who sing of thee worthy of the Heavenly Kingdom.[^6849]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, illumine in love, I pray Thee, the souls of them that rise early unto Thee, that they may know Thee, O Word of God, as the true God, calling them out of the darkness of sin.[^6850]

*Troparion 1:* With the divine beam of Him Who shone forth from thy loins, enlighten my soul that sleeps in darkness, O Virgin, and drive away the gloom of sin, enlightening me with thy light.[^6851]

*Troparion 2:* Heal my soul that hath fallen among thieves, O Good One, mercifully dropping the oil and wine of thy tenderheartedness, unstintingly granting understanding unto me.[^6852]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Heal the bodily and spiritual ailments in us who have recourse unto thee, O All-Hymned One; for thou art strength unto those who run under the shelter of thy prayer, O Mother of God.[^6853]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* The Divine Holy Spirit, having made His abode in thy womb, O most holy and pure one...

...and overshadowing thee, showed thee forth as the house of the Trinity: who by the good pleasure of the Father didst conceive the Word and didst give birth to Him.[^6854]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, having fled unto Thy calm haven, I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Abundantly Merciful One.[^6855]

*Troparion 1:* Dispel the darkness of my wretched soul, and enlighten me with the light, O All-Pure One, that shone forth from thy womb and illumined the whole of human nature.[^6856]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me from the plot of enemies visible and invisible, O Pure One, and grant me the mighty shelter of thy protection, for unto thee, O Virgin, have I now fled.[^6857]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* He Who created all things was incarnate in the last days from thine all-holy womb; and having restored human nature which from Adam had grievously fallen, He renewed the world.[^6858]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* The splendor of the holy Angels sings of thee, and I pray thee, O All-Immaculate One: Take away from me the unseemly fantasies of demons, preserving my heart in tranquility.[^6859]

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:* The assaults of the adversaries war against thine inheritance, O pure Lady: destroy their deceits, and cast down to the earth their haughty insolence; for thou art the protectress of those who pray unto thee in faith.[^6860]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Angel made the furnace pour down dew upon the holy children, but the command of God consuming the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6861]

*Troparion 1:* Having enlightened me who am in the darkness of folly with unapproachable light, O All-Pure One, guide me unto the light, that I may cry in faith and piety unto thy Son: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^6862]

*Troparion 2:* Drive away the darkened cloud from my soul, O Mother of God, illuminating me with the light of the Creator of all; for thou art the receptacle of the pre-eternal Light Who shone forth impassibly from the Father.[^6863]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Incline Thyself unto our supplications, O pure Mother of God. Be moved mercifully in prayer toward God, and make Him merciful unto us who ever hope in thee, O Good One.[^6864]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* Defiled as I am by the passions of wicked deeds, a propitiation...

...grant me, O Pure One, drops of tears, washing away the wicked shame of my sin, the fountain of wretchedness.[^6865]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew upon the holy ones, and the sacrifice of the righteous one Thou didst consume with water; for Thou, O Christ, doest all things by the mere act of Thy will: Thee do we exalt supremely unto all ages.[^6866]

*Troparion 1:* O Maiden who in the flesh gavest birth unto the Master of all, snatch me from the violence of passions, and unite me in love unto the Creator of all things, who sing of Him unto all ages.[^6867]

*Troparion 2:* When I lie bedridden in affliction, raise me up by thy visitation, O Maiden, and grant me health of soul and body, who sing of Christ unto the ages.[^6868]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Quench the flame of my passions by the dew of thy prayers, O Lady, and save me, even as formerly thy Son delivered the youths who supremely exalted Him unto the ages.[^6869]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* I fear not the howling of the adversary, having in thee the hope of my expectation, O Good One; for thou dost ever protect and save from tribulations those who praise thee.[^6870]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is not possible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure One, the incarnate Word hath appeared unto men; Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^6871]

*Troparion 1:* Deliver from all evils him that flees unto thee in faith, O all-pure Virgin; and grant health and deliverance from tribulations unto me who have fled under thy shelter, O Maiden, that in thankful voices I may ever glorify thee.[^6872]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast destroyed the curse of sorrow, O Pure One, and poured forth joy unto the world by thy childbirth, having given birth unto the Fountain of blessing: therefore praising thee, the All-Hymned One, all we faithful call thee blessed in thanksgiving for ever.[^6873]

*Glory... Troparion 3:* Thy womb became the receptacle of Light, O Mother of God, All-Hymned One, of Him Who shone forth from the Father before all ages: therefore all of us, glorifying thee as the Mother of God, magnify Him as thy Son and God.[^6874]

*Both now... Troparion 4:* The Son Who before time shone forth from the Father, thou alone, O Pure One, didst bear in time by the descent of the Divine Spirit: therefore thee, the All-...

...pure Mother of God who knewest not man, all we faithful confess with tongue and heart.[^6875]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion Prayers; after Our Father: Troparion and the rest as usual.*[^6876]

*On Monday at Matins:*
*After the 1st Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*[^6877]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I ponder the dread day, and weep over my wicked deeds: how shall I answer the immortal King? And with what boldness shall I, the prodigal, look upon the Judge? O Compassionate Father, Only-Begotten Son, and Holy Spirit, have mercy upon me.[^6878]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^6879]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* In the valley of weeping, in the place which Thou hast appointed, when Thou, O Merciful One, shalt sit to execute righteous judgment, expose not my secret things, neither put me to shame before the Angels; but spare me, O God, and have mercy upon me.[^6880]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* The most glorious Mother of God, who is holier than the holy Angels, let us praise unceasingly with heart and mouth, confessing her as the Mother of God, who truly gave birth unto God incarnate, and who prays without ceasing for our souls.[^6881]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*[^6882]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Have mercy upon us, O Lord, have mercy upon us; for being destitute of all defense, we sinners offer this supplication unto Thee as Master: Have mercy upon us.[^6883]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^6884]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord, have mercy upon us, for in Thee have we hoped; be not exceedingly wroth with us, neither remember our iniquities; but look upon us even now, as Thou art compassionate, and deliver us from our enemies; for Thou art our God, and we are Thy people, all the works of Thy hands, and we call upon Thy name.[^6885]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^6886]

*Martyrikon:* Light ever dawns for the righteous; for the Saints, having been enlightened in Thee, ever shine as luminaries, having extinguished the lamp of the unfaithful. By their prayers, O our Savior, enlighten Thou my lamp, O Lord, and save me.[^6887]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Standing in the temple of thy glory, we seem to stand in heaven...

...O Mother of God, heavenly gate: open unto us the doors of thy mercy.[^6888]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "The Hope" (Оу҆пова́нїе):*[^6889]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I shudder at the coming of Thy dread day, O Christ, and I fear and tremble before the inexorable judgment seat, as having a multitude of transgressions; but before the end turn me back and save me, as Thou art merciful God, by the prayers of Thine Angels, O compassionate Savior, Thou only Lover of mankind.[^6890]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O my soul, spending thy whole life in slothfulness, thou takest not to mind the terrible day of judgment. Arouse thyself therefore, and choose repentance; turn back and cry aloud unto Christ: O Merciful One, remember not our many transgressions in the hour of condemnation.[^6891]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* O Hope and Haven of them that trust in thee, O good Mother of God, protection of the world! Pray earnestly together with the Bodiless Powers unto Him to Whom thou didst give birth, the God Who loves mankind, that our souls may be delivered from every threatening, O only Blessed One.[^6892]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His holy Martyrs.*
*(Whose acrostic is: Accept, O Christ, the weeping of mine eyes). A composition of Joseph.*[^6893]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The sensory Pharaoh was engulfed with all his host, but Israel, having passed through the midst of the sea, cried aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord our God, for He hath been glorified.[^6894]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*

*Troparion 1:* Having fallen, O Christ, into the hands of wicked thieves, and having been made half-dead by soul-corrupting wounds, heal me with the oil of mercy and have compassion on me, that I may glorify Thee.[^6895]

*Troparion 2:* Having sinned greatly, I became a den of thieves; O Christ Who wast born in a cave, grant me a cloud of tears and cleanse me, that I may be a temple unto Thy Holy Spirit.[^6896]

*Martyrikon:* Having youthfully cast down the enemy, O sufferers, ye were truly adorned with crowns woven by God; and ye stand before God in the highest with the Angels, filled with glory.[^6897]

*Martyrikon:* Having formerly drowned the spiritual Pharaoh in the depth of your blood, O venerable Great-Martyrs, ye passed over unto the heavenly land of promise, glorifying Christ.[^6898]

*Theotokion:* With the Martyrs, O Pure One, with the divine Prophets, and with all the Angels, pray unto the Creator of all, that He may save those who in faith glorify and magnify thee.[^6899]

*Another Canon, to the Holy Heavenly Bodiless Angels.*
*(Whose acrostic is: The sixth singing of the noetic hosts). A composition of Theophanes.*[^6900]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Traversing the deep on foot as if on dry land, Israel, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh...

...engulfed, cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^6901]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Praising the fair splendor of the heavenly choirs, I cry aloud to be enlightened by their luminous lightnings, praying unto Thee, O Christ, the only Compassionate One.[^6902]

*Troparion 2:* Having adorned all things by Thy Word, and the Angelic choirs by Thine all-holy Spirit proceeding from Thee, Thou didst order them to sing of Thee Who art the Cause of all, as Thou art almighty.[^6903]

*Theotokion:* Having received thy Creator as He Himself willed, incarnate beyond understanding from thy seedless womb, O Pure One, thou hast truly been shown forth as the Lady of creation.[^6904]

*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos:* Having founded the thought of my soul upon the firm rock of Thy faith, establish me, O Lord; for I have Thee, O Good One, as refuge and establishment.[^6905]

*Troparion 1:* I promise to repent unto Christ, yet in no wise do I depart from evil deeds: what shall become of me, enveloped in such great blindness? O Son of God, have Thou compassion upon me.[^6906]

*Troparion 2:* I reflect upon the longsuffering of God, having spent my life in slothfulness, wretch that I am; and I fear lest the visitation of death suddenly overtake me.[^6907]

*Martyrikon:* Desiring persecutions, pains, and perils even as food, the Martyrs rejoiced, beholding with noetic eyes the everlasting enjoyment.[^6908]

*Martyrikon:* Having most wisely established your mind in fortitude as upon the firm rock of piety, O holy Martyrs, ye feared not the manifold wounds.[^6909]

*Theotokion:* A new Paradise hast thou been revealed unto us, O Pure One, having in the midst the Tree of Life: eating of Which, O All-Pure One, Adam is released from the evil putting-to-death.[^6910]
