*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* There is none holy like unto Thee, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^6911]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* The Nature Which by Its will made every rational nature out of nothing is praised in three Persons with thrice-holy voices and is faithfully glorified.[^6912]

*Troparion 2:* Pouring forth what is good, O Master of all, and richly shedding it abroad, by communion of good things Thou hast shown forth the bodiless Angelic choirs, O Lover of mankind.[^6913]

*Theotokion:* Forgiveness hath been given unto us for thy sake, O Mother of God; for the Master of the Law, having become incarnate of thee and suffered the passions for our sake, hath delivered all.[^6914]

*Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thy virtue, O Christ, hath covered the heavens, and all things are filled with Thy praise, O Lord.[^6915]

*Troparion 1:* Condemn us not, who have sinned much against Thee; but in Thine accustomed mercy have compassion, O Master.[^6916]

*Troparion 2:* Being the Way of life, O Savior, vouchsafe me ever to turn aside from the deadly ways of sin.[^6917]

*Martyrikon:* By your burning wounds ye quenched the fire, O suffering Martyrs; by the dew of the Divine Spirit ye remained unconsumed.[^6918]

*Martyrikon:* Ye delight on high in the torrent of divine delight, O Martyrs, having richly inherited it by streams of blood.[^6919]

*Theotokion:* Having spent my life in slothfulness, O All-Pure One, I flee unto thy protection: have compassion, and save me.[^6920]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, my God and Lord, the venerable Church godly sings, crying aloud from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^6921]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Possessing a nature incapable of corruption, the most divine minds stand round about Thy venerable throne, having inherited Thee, the Fountain of immortality, O Lover of mankind.[^6922]

*Troparion 2:* Sanctified by the Holy Spirit, the Angelic assemblies remain unmoved unto evil, being deified by ascents toward the Primal Good.[^6923]

*Theotokion:* The curse of our first mother hath been abolished for thy sake, O all-immaculate Mother of God; for thou, O All-Pure One, hast given birth unto the ever-flowing Fountain of blessing.[^6924]

*Tone 6. Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Christ Who hast shone forth light unto the world, enlighten my heart that cries unto Thee out of the night, and save me.[^6925]

*Troparion 1:* O Christ Who didst spring forth from the root of Jesse, dry up the sproutings of my passions, planting Thy fear within me.[^6926]

*Troparion 2:* Let us acquire sighing like the publican, and let us depart from evil deeds, that we may escape the everlasting sighing.[^6927]

*Martyrikon:* Wounded by divine longing for Thee, Thy Martyrs, O Lord, when wounded by torments, rejoiced.[^6928]

*Martyrikon:* Suffering in the whole body, ye Martyrs cried aloud, and Christ heard your voice.[^6929]

*Theotokion:* Let us hymn the Mother of God, the heavenly gate, through which all sinners enter unto the portals of forgiveness.[^6930]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, enlighten with love, I pray, the souls of those who rise early unto Thee, O Word of God, to know Thee, the true God, calling them from the darkness of sin.[^6931]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Beholding Thee seated upon the Cherubic throne, O all-seeing Creator and God, the Prophet was taught by signs Thy dominion and Lordship.[^6932]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding Thee surrounded, O Master, by myriad myriads and thousands of thousands of Angels, joined unto human form, Daniel was mystically taught the might of Thy glory.[^6933]

*Theotokion:* Fair in beauty did Thy Son appear beyond the sons of earthly men, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady; for He is God Who is over all, even though He took human nature from thee.[^6934]

*Tone 6. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Swallowed by the whale of sin, I cry unto Thee, O Christ: As Thou didst the Prophet, deliver me from corruption.[^6935]

*Troparion 1:* O Christ Who didst give sight unto the blind, open the eyes of my soul, that I may see Thy light and be delivered from the darkness of passions.[^6936]

*Troparion 2:* Fill my soul with the lifegiving waters of repentance, O Compassionate One, granting me rivers of tears.[^6937]

*Martyrikon:* The valleys of the Martyrs bud forth lilies of healings, and sweeten the hearts of all the pious.[^6938]

*Martyrikon:* When dissolved by torments from the corruptible body, O Holy Ones, ye were bound in heart and love unto the Master.[^6939]

*Theotokion:* O holy Mother of God, cleanse with thy prayers my heart that is defiled by unclean deeds.[^6940]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Plenteous in mercy.[^6941]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Beholding in purity of mind Thy radiant Angels descending upon the ladder, O Master, Jacob was most manifestly taught from afar Thy coming in the flesh.[^6942]

*Troparion 2:* Having understood the divine, radiant host of the Angels, O Master, Israel Thy servant rejoiced, seeing them keeping festival in good order and standing around Thine ineffable glory.[^6943]

*Theotokion:* Truly higher than the heavenly mountains, the Angelic Powers, wast thou shown forth, O manifest mountain, Maiden Sovereign Lady, who didst contain the ray of the Godhead.[^6944]

*Tone 6. Ode 7. Heirmos:* Having heard the song of Thy holy Children, and having bedewed the burning furnace: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, the God of our fathers.[^6945]

*Troparion 1:* By exceeding malice have I been blinded in mind, and looked not upon the light of repentance; O Christ my Savior, save me.[^6946]

*Troparion 2:* I hope in Thy loving-kindness, I fall down before Thee, O Compassionate One, and ask remission of my sins: despise me not, O Master.[^6947]

*Martyrikon:* O Thou Who didst clothe with transcendent glory Thy Holy Ones who suffered in the world, by their prayers, O my Christ, save me.[^6948]

*Martyrikon:* Ye spat upon the unrighteous decrees, being divine keepers of the righteous law, having lawfully suffered through faith.[^6949]

*Theotokion:* Thou givest birth, O Maiden, unto the Master Who took the form of a servant; pray Him, O Pure One, to deliver me from the servitude of passions.[^6950]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* An Angel made the furnace dew-bearing unto the holy Children, but the burning command of God consuming the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^6951]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Ye shine in beauty, all ye holy Angels of the Almighty, drawing nigh unto the ineffable glory of Christ, and ever singing unto Him with love: Blessed art Thou, O God, unto the ages.[^6952]

*Troparion 2:* Revolving spiritually around Thee, O Master, the Angels ever-existingly receive Thine incomprehensible shining-forth of light, ever singing and saying: Blessed art Thou, O God, unto the ages.[^6953]

*Theotokion:* The Lord is with thee, O Blessed One, said the bodiless Archangel; for desiring to renew corrupt nature, He dwelt in thy womb...

...O Pure One, blessed Maiden who in the flesh gavest birth unto God.[^6954]

*Tone 6. Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace Thy holy Children imitated the Cherubim, chanting the thrice-holy hymn: Bless, sing, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^6955]

*Troparion 1:* In manner I have emulated the pitilessness of the rich man, neglecting my mind that is impoverished and suppurating with wickedness before the doors of repentance: O Christ, send me not into the unquenchable fire.[^6956]

*Troparion 2:* In winter and on the sabbath of idle works, take me not unprepared from life, O Compassionate One Who didst calm the winter of sin; but grant me a divine turning.[^6957]

*Martyrikon:* The Martyrs, trampling underfoot deceit by unfeigned faith, endured every tempest of torments, blessing, singing, and supremely exalting Christ unto the ages.[^6958]

*Martyrikon:* Having quenched the burning flame of torments by the dew of endurance, O Holy Ones, ye burned up the matter of godlessness, burning with zeal of divine love, O gloriously triumphant Martyrs.[^6959]

*Theotokion:* Being more honorable than the Angels, thou didst give birth unto the all-holy God, having been exceedingly sanctified, O Virgin Theotokos; therefore sanctify my soul.[^6960]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and didst consume the sacrifice and the water of the righteous one; for Thou, O Christ, dost do all things merely by willing: Thee do we supremely exalt unto all ages.[^6961]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Even as of old Thou didst surround Elisha Thy servant with bodiless hosts: likewise even now, O Christ, surround Thy Church that supremely exalts Thee unto the ages.[^6962]

*Troparion 2:* O divine Archistrategoi, standing before the dread throne, pray that they may be delivered from transgressions who with love glorify and supremely exalt Christ unto all ages.[^6963]

*Theotokion:* Unto thee as Mother and Virgin we cry, Rejoice, together with divine Gabriel, O thou who hast been made glad of God; for thou didst bear in the flesh for us the Word of God, Whom we supremely exalt unto all ages.[^6964]

*Canticle of the Theotokos: My soul doth magnify the Lord.*
*Tone 6. Ode 9. Heirmos:* O Virgin who didst receive from the Angel the Rejoice, and gavest birth unto Thine own Creator: save those who magnify thee.[^6965]

*Troparion 1:* Joseph, indeed, having kept his chastity, was recognized as the dispenser of wheat; but I, having lived prodigally, am held fast by hunger for virtuous deeds.[^6966]

*Troparion 2:* Having repented like Peter, O Christ, I offer tears; like the publican I sigh; like the prodigal I cry from the deep: I have sinned, forgive me.[^6967]

*Martyrikon:* By the help of Christ, O All-Wise Ones, having overthrown the malice of the enemy, ye received from on high the crowns of victory.[^6968]

*Martyrikon:* The holy memory of the Martyrs sanctifies by the Holy Spirit all who celebrate it with right belief.[^6969]

*Theotokion:* Enlighten me who am darkened by sins, O thou who didst bear the Light of impassibility, that I may hymn thee, O pure Ever-Virgin.[^6970]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos:* It is not possible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure One, the incarnate Word appeared unto men; Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^6971]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* As Thou didst make the Angel striking Thy people with the sword cease for David Thy prophet: as Thou art compassionate, O Christ, grant peace unto all Churches, lightening the temptations that now beset them, as Thou art plenteous in mercy.[^6972]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding the affliction of Thy people, O Christ, as the Physician of souls and bodies, heal them, O Master, by the supplications of Thy ministers who now stand round about Thee, the King of all, and who with unceasing voices glorify Thee as God.[^6973]

*Theotokion:* Principalities, Archangels, Dominions, and Seraphim, Powers, Authorities, and Angels, Thrones with the Cherubim, honoring now thy most glorified childbearing, reverently and ever glorify thee, O Virgin Mother.[^6974]

*It is truly meet: Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^6975]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 6:*[^6976]

*Sticheron 1:* Having found me stripped of virtues, the enemy wounded me with the arrow of sin; but do Thou, as Physician of souls and bodies, heal the wounds of my soul, O God, and have mercy upon me.[^6977]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^6978]

*Sticheron 2:* Heal the bruises of my heart, O Savior, that have grown upon me from many sins, as Thou art the Physician of souls and bodies, ever granting remission of sins unto them that ask; grant me tears of repentance, and vouchsafe unto me the forgiveness of debts, O Lord, and have mercy upon me.[^6979]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and order the works of our hands upon us; yea, order the work of our hands.*[^6980]

*Martyrikon:* O Lord, in the memory of Thy Saints, all creation keeps festival; the heavens rejoice with the Angels, and the earth makes merry with men; by their prayers have mercy upon us.[^6981]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Thou didst receive the Archangel's word, and wast shown forth as the Cherubic throne; and upon thine arms, O Mother of God, thou didst bear the Hope of our souls.[^6982]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion Prayers; after Our Father: Troparion, Litany, and the rest.*[^6983]

*On Monday, The Beatitudes, Tone 6:*[^6984]

*Troparion 1:* Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom, and save me as Thou alone art the Lover of mankind.[^6985]

*Troparion 2:* Overlook my deeds committed knowingly and unknowingly, O Jesus Lover of mankind, and vouchsafe me the portion of those being saved.[^6986]

*Troparion 3:* O Christ Who didst enlighten the Angelic choirs, by their supplications enlighten the sight of my heart.[^6987]

*Troparion 4:* Having endured every ordeal of wounds, O passion-bearers, ye ever heal the wounds and bruises of men who believe.[^6988]

*Glory...* Let us worship the Holy Trinity, praised by the Angelic hosts, crying unto It: Save our souls.[^6989]

*Both now...* Deliver me from eternal fire and from the torments that await me, O Birthgiver of God, that I may call thee blessed.[^6990]

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner

*On Monday Evening:*[^6991]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Despaired of on account of her life" (Ѿча́ѧннаѧ житїѧ̀ ра́ди):*[^6992]

*Sticheron 1:* Who will not weep for me, who have transgressed the commandment of the Most High through intemperance? I have made my dwelling in hades instead of Paradise; through the seeing of sweet food...

...I procured death, and on its account became a stranger unto God's glory and life; but accept me who repent, O Lord, as Thou art merciful and the Lover of mankind, for the sake of Thy great mercy.[^6993]

*Sticheron 2:* Behold my grief and pain, and the measureless multitude of my transgressions, the affliction of my soul, and the deception of my mind; perceive the voice of him that is despaired of and condemned; and give me, O Lord, a contrite spirit and a humble heart, and grant a fountain of tears and forgiveness of my many transgressions, for the sake of Thy great mercy.[^6994]

*Sticheron 3:* O God Who desirest that all men should be saved, look down and hear my prayer, and cast not away my tears as vanity. For who ever came unto Thee weeping and was not straightway saved? Or who ever cried fervently unto Thee and was not straightway heard? But, O Master, Thou art found swift unto the salvation of all who pray unto Thee, as Thou art invincible in mercy.[^6995]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Forerunner John:*[^6996]

*Sticheron 4:* Despise me not who have gone astray in the path of life, O Forerunner of the Savior: for I lift up the eyes of my heart unto thee; and set the feet of my soul upon the rock of repentance. Show me the straight path to walk, that leads into the salvific gates; and turn not away the groaning of my sorrowing heart, O most excellent of all born of women.[^6997]

*Sticheron 5:* O Baptist and Forerunner of the Savior, be a helper unto me, O Blessed One, who am stuck fast in the mire of unclean thoughts; stretch forth unto me the hand that touched the incorrupt Crown; strengthen me manfully to do the works of repentance which thou didst proclaim: and vouchsafe me thy servant to be within the Kingdom which thou didst first teach.[^6998]

*Sticheron 6:* O thou who by thy birth didst loose thy mother's barrenness and thy father's muteness, loose the fruitlessness of my childless heart, and quickly take away all irrationality of soul: for thou art the voice of the Word crying repentance; vouchsafe me, who through slothfulness have become estranged from God, ever to fulfill this, O Baptist of Christ.[^6999]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Show forth upon me, the wretched one, the abyss of thy love for mankind, the depth of thy loving-kindness, and the unnumbered compassions of thy goodness, O all-pure Virgin. Calm the pasture of sin, granting chastity, and preserve undefiled my body together with my soul, O thou who didst give birth unto the Savior.[^7000]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord:*[^7001]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 6:*[^7002]

*Sticheron 1:* At Thy dread coming, O Christ, let us not hear: "I know you not." For in Thee, O Savior, have we placed our hope, even though through our negligence we have not kept Thy commandments; but spare our souls, we pray.[^7003]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^7004]

*Sticheron 2:* I have not acquired repentance, nor yet tears; therefore I pray Thee, O Christ God: Before the end grant me to turn and give me compunction, that I may be delivered from torment.[^7005]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^7006]

*Martyrikon:* The Passion-bearing Martyrs, heavenly citizens, having suffered upon earth, endured many torments; by their prayers and intercession, O Lord, preserve us all.[^7007]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* No one who flees unto thee goes away from thee confounded, O all-pure Virgin Theotokos! But he asks grace and receives the gift according to what is profitable in his petition.[^7008]

*Now lettest Thou: Trisagion Prayers; after Our Father: Troparion, Litany, and Dismissal.*[^7009]

*On Monday at Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 6:*[^7010]

*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Traversing the deep on foot as if on dry land, Israel, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh engulfed, cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^7011]

*Refrain: Most Holy Sovereign Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^7012]

*Troparion 1:* Bowed down by a multitude of temptations and grievous deeds, I bend the neck of my soul and body unto thee, O Pure One, and fervently cry unto thee: Do thou set me upright.[^7013]

*Troparion 2:* O all-immaculate Maiden, strong help of the faithful and hope of Christians! Deliver me from carnal pleasures and passions that war against me.[^7014]

*Glory...* O Guide of light unto them in darkness, who didst bear the Noetic Light, O All-Pure One, enlighten my soul and sanctify my mind, dispelling the darkness of passions and transgressions.[^7015]

*Both now...* Dispel the darkness of my darkened soul caused by the assaults of unfitting thoughts, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos, with the light of the holy commandments...

...of Him Who was incarnate of thee.[^7016]

*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy like unto Thee, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^7017]

*Troparion 1:* Having hoped in thee, O Pure and All-Pure One, let me not fall away from my expectation toward thee; as the Mother of God, the merciful Lover of mankind, deliver me from the snares of the enemy.[^7018]

*Troparion 2:* O Mary, Mother of God, gate of salvation through which the Creator of all alone passed: open unto me now the divine gates of repentance, O All-Pure One.[^7019]

*Glory...* Be thou a haven and guardian unto me, O Pure One, who am ever tempest-tossed upon the sea of life by the grievous billows of passions, O only Ever-Virgin.[^7020]

*Both now...* O Mary, Mother of God, enlighten my soul which is grievously darkened by many sins, wounded by the arrow of the crafty one, and grown feeble.[^7021]

*Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, my God and Lord, the venerable Church godly sings, crying aloud from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^7022]

*Troparion 1:* Being my strength, O All-Pure One, and my refuge and unconquerable wall and intercessor before God: deliver me from the eternal flame and Gehenna.[^7023]

*Troparion 2:* O Mary, all-pure one,[^7024] dispel the tumult of the passions of my mind and the storm of temptations, O thou who didst give birth unto the Fountain of impassibility, O Virgin Mother.

*Glory...* O pure and all-immaculate one, who wast the spacious receptacle of purity for the divine dwelling: consume the defilement and impurity of my soul.[^7025]

*Both now...* Thee, the only all-pure, the only undefiled one, I entreat, O Maiden: Cleanse with thy prayers my soul that is defiled by pleasurable passions and mired in filth.[^7026]

*Tone 6. Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, enlighten with love, I pray, the souls of those who rise early unto Thee, O Word of God, to know Thee, the true God, calling them from the darkness of sin.[^7027]

*Troparion 1:* Defiled in thought and mired by many fallings, O Mother of God, I entreat thee, the only undefiled one: Have compassion and save me by thine intercession.[^7028]

*Troparion 2:* With thy divine radiance, O Good One, enlighten my soul that is darkened by pleasures, and direct it unto the path of salvation, O thou who alone didst give birth unto Christ the Savior.[^7029]

*Glory...* Rend asunder my bonds of sin by thy mediation and protection, O Virgin; fill my wretched soul with the joy of divine tranquility, and deliver me from darkness.[^7030]

*Both now...* Having now acquired thee alone as a divine refuge, O Good One, I cry unto thee and faithfully fall down: Be thou my protection and shelter of salvation, O Sovereign Lady of the world, and save me.[^7031]

*Tone 6. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Plenteous in mercy.[^7032]

*Troparion 1:* Being guilty of sins and grievously wounded, wretch that I am, I flee unto thee, the merciful Mother of God, praying: Destroy the assaults of my transgressions.[^7033]

*Troparion 2:* O gate of grace, who didst open the heavenly door unto the faithful: open unto me the radiant door of repentance, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me from the gates of death.[^7034]

*Glory...* Strengthen me who am bent down by passions through the assaults of adversaries, O Mother of God, who didst bear the Fountain of impassibility; for unto thee have I fled, the comforter of my wretched soul.[^7035]

*Both now...* Look down, O Sovereign Lady, upon me, the humble one, and beyond hope save me; for thou art my hope and protection, life and light unto my heart, O Mother of God, and my establishment.[^7036]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:* Thou art truly the strength of the helpless, O all-holy Virgin Mother; therefore we the humble are lifted up through thee and held high in thee: thou art the protection of all and intercessor before God.[^7037]

*Tone 6. Ode 7. Heirmos:* An Angel made the furnace dew-bearing unto the holy Children, but the burning command of God consuming the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^7038]

*Troparion 1:* We have thee ever as a purification before God, O All-Immaculate One; therefore ask deliverance for all, O All-Pure One, from the dread torments there...

...who truly confess thee as the Mother of God.[^7039]

*Troparion 2:* Raise me up who am held fast by slothfulness unto the doing of divine deeds, O All-Pure One, strengthening me against the enemies that ever grievously war against me and beguile me with contrary thoughts.[^7040]

*Glory...* Leave me not to perish, O Virgin, nor suffer me to become food for the fierce serpent that ever thirsts for my destruction; but grant me rich mercy.[^7041]

*Both now...* Cease not to entreat God our Lover of mankind, O All-Immaculate One, that we may receive perfect forgiveness, O Pure One, and attain the prepared heavenly good things and joy that cannot be taken away.[^7042]

*Tone 6. Ode 8. Heirmos:* From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and didst consume the sacrifice and the water of the righteous one; for Thou, O Christ, dost do all things merely by willing: Thee do we supremely exalt unto all ages.[^7043]

*Troparion 1:* Do thou, O Virgin, by thy childbearing, O all-pure one, show my barren heart fruitful in good works, entreating God.[^7044]

*Troparion 2:* Offer supplication unto God, O Virgin, to save our souls, O full of God's grace, and deliver us from the reception of passions, from the evil temptation of demons.[^7045]

*Glory...* I flee unto thy protection, O All-Immaculate One, and now set thee forth as the defender of my life: deliver me, O Maiden, from the dread judgment, and the trial, and the eternal fire.[^7046]

*Both now...* Strengthen my soul that is shaken by the malice of the adversary, O pure Virgin, and rescue me from fiery torment and the painful portion of the goats.[^7047]

*Tone 6. Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is not possible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure One, the incarnate Word appeared unto men; Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^7048]

*Troparion 1:* The surging of passions troubles me and whelms me with pleasures, O all-immaculate Virgin; who didst bear the Helmsman Christ, stretch forth unto me the hand of help and save me, the only salvation of those who in faith call thee blessed.[^7049]

*Troparion 2:* O bridal chamber and throne of the King, mountain of God, and chosen city, and paradise, all-radiant cloud of the Sun! Enlighten my soul, driving away the cloud of sin...

...sins, O thou who art full of God's grace.[^7050]

*Glory...* O pure Virgin made glad of God, gate of light: open the doors of my soul, shutting fast the entrance of sin, that the hand of the crafty one may not lay hold of me, leading me grievously unto all-destructive and dread torment.[^7051]

*Both now...* Awaken, O my soul, and watch in prayers and in all-good cares, driving away the sleep of despondency, having ever as a vigilant intercessor the pure and all-merciful Mother of God.[^7052]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion Prayers; after Our Father: Troparion and the rest.*[^7053]

*On Tuesday at Matins:*

*After the First Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I meditate upon the dread day, and weep over my wicked deeds: How shall I answer the immortal King? And with what boldness shall I, a prodigal, look upon the Judge? O Compassionate Father, Only-begotten Son, and Holy Spirit, have mercy upon me.[^7054]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^7055]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* In the valley of weeping, in the place which Thou hast appointed, when Thou, O Merciful One, sittest to execute righteous judgment, expose not my hidden deeds, neither put me to shame before the Angels; but spare me, O God, and have mercy upon me.[^7056]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Open the doors of tender mercy unto us, O blessed Virgin Theotokos, that we who hope in thee may not perish, but may be delivered through thee from tribulations; for thou art the salvation of the Christian race.[^7057]

*After the Second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* O my soul, why dost thou in despondency serve sin? And why being sick dost thou not come unto the Physician? Arise henceforth from the evil things which thou hast committed, and cry aloud unto the Savior, saying: O Hope of the hopeless, Life of the despaired, O Savior, raise me up and save me.[^7058]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^7059]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Grant me, O Lord, the vigilance of the wise virgins, and enlighten the lamp of my soul with the oil of Thy compassions, that I may sing the Angelic song unto Thee: Alleluia.[^7060]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^7061]

*Martyrikon:* Having endured the passion-bearing struggle, O Saints, they received from Thee the honors of victory; they brought to nought the devices of the lawless, and received incorruptible crowns. Being entreated for their sake, O God, grant us great mercy.[^7062]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* O Hope of the world, good Virgin Theotokos, we ask thine and only dread intercession: Have compassion upon a helpless people; entreat the merciful God that our souls may be delivered from every threatening, O only Blessed One.[^7063]

*After the Third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having laid all their hope" (Всѐ возло́жше):*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* As thou didst show unto the people the ways of life, O John, crying aloud: "Make straight your hearts for the Lord"; save my most sinful soul, lead unto compunction my petrified mind, and deliver me from future torment by thy prayers, O Forerunner of the Savior.[^7064]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The storm of passions and the fierce surging of transgressions have drowned me in the depth of despair, O Word; but stretch forth unto me Thy mighty right hand, and save me as Thou didst Peter, by the sacred prayers of Thy Forerunner, from the depth of my fiercely wicked iniquities.[^7065]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Him Who before the ages was begotten of the Father without a mother, the Son and Word of God, in these latter times hast thou, O Birthgiver of Good, brought forth incarnate from thy pure blood without a man; Him do thou entreat, to grant us forgiveness of sins before the end.[^7066]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to the Holy Martyrs. By Lord Joseph. Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Heirmos:* He was unto me a helper and protector for salvation; this is my God, and I will glorify Him; the God of my father, and I will exalt Him: for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^7067]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*

*Troparion 1:* O my Redeemer Jesus, as Thou didst once redeem from many transgressions the harlot who repented well, I pray Thee: deliver me also from my countless evils, as Thou art merciful.[^7068]

*Troparion 2:* Sailing through this fierce deep in the vain contemplation of life, O Jesus, I have fallen into many drownings; deliver me from them and save me.[^7069]

*Martyrikon:* Let us joyfully honor the valiant sufferers, the destroyers of the alien, who patiently suffered and received from God the crowns of victory.[^7070]

*Martyrikon:* Speaking with wisdom through the boldness of God the Word, [ye overcame] the evil rhetors...

...ye overcame, O wise ones; and having endured every kind of wound, ye were exceedingly glorified.[^7071]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as the gate of life, having shut the gates of death by thy childbearing, O Pure One; thou wast shown forth as the chosen earth, through which human nature was lifted up from earth unto heaven.[^7072]

*Another Canon, to the Holy Forerunner John. By Joseph. Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Heirmos:* The sensible Pharaoh was drowned with all his host, but Israel, passing through the midst of the sea, cried aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord our God, for He hath been glorified.[^7073]

*Troparion 1:* A divine Angel foretold thy birth unto thy father, O Angel of God; together with him remember us, that we may find mercy in the day of judgment, O holy Forerunner.[^7074]

*Troparion 2:* Being a fair offshoot of the wilderness, O Forerunner of Christ, uproot from the core the slothfulness that unceasingly shoots up in me, and make me bring forth fruits of repentance.[^7075]

*Troparion 3:* A barren womb most gloriously brought thee forth as fruit, making fruitful the hearts that before were unfruitful; but in faith I cry unto thee, O Baptist: Uproot my fruitless thoughts.[^7076]

*Theotokion:* By deceit the crafty enemy unceasingly hunts me; snatch me from his snaring, O All-Immaculate One, and guide me, O Theotokos, to do the Master's divine will.[^7077]

*Tone 6. Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish upon the rock of Thy commandments, O Lord, my wavering heart, for Thou alone art holy and Lord.[^7078]

*Troparion 1:* Look down upon me with Thy merciful eye, when I am about to stand before Thee and be judged, O Lord Who alone art easily reconciled.[^7079]

*Troparion 2:* Receive me as Thou didst the Publican, sighing from the depth of soul; and grant me repentance, O Savior, delivering me from every sin.[^7080]

*Martyrikon:* By the flow of your all-holy blood, O Saints, the abominable blood ceased that was once offered unto demons in idol temples.[^7081]

*Martyrikon:* Strengthened by the All-Holy Spirit, O Saints, ye cast down the destructive idol shrines, establishing all in the faith.[^7082]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst conceive, O Most Holy One, Him Who holds the whole world; therefore I pray thee: Deliver me from every torment that holds me fast.[^7083]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* Having founded the thought of my soul upon the firm rock of Thy faith, establish me, O Lord; for I have Thee, O Good One, as refuge and establishment.[^7084]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me now return from the straying way, and stretch forth thy hand unto me, O blessed Forerunner, who am ever sailing in the deep of evils.[^7085]

*Troparion 2:* I live slothfully, and the cutting down draws near; by thy prayers grant me revival, O ever-glorious Forerunner, that I may not be sent into the unquenchable fire as unfruitful.[^7086]

*Troparion 3:* The dread day is at the doors, and I am compassed about with grievous burdens; relieve me from them, O Baptist of the Lord, by thy pure supplications.[^7087]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as the throne of God, O Theotokos, whereon Christ, sitting in the flesh, raised up men from the first fall, who sing unto thee with joyful voices.[^7088]

*Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos:* The Prophet heard of Thy coming, O Lord, and was afraid, that Thou wast about to be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I heard report of Thee and was afraid; glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^7089]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me streams of tears drying up the fountains of my passions, and washing away all mire of sin, O Compassionate and Plenteous in mercy, and quenching the eternal, unquenchable flame of Gehenna's fire.[^7090]

*Troparion 2:* Unceasingly I scratch the sores of my soul with love of pleasure, and remain unhealed, not willing myself to come to my senses. What will become of me? And what shall I do? O compassionate Christ, heal and save me.[^7091]

*Martyrikon:* Having by divine governance through faith sailed across the unstable deep of destructive waters and the storm of malice, O ever-blessed Martyrs, ye reached the distress-free and stormless haven of the heavenly Kingdom.[^7092]

*Martyrikon:* Having become illuminators of piety and luminaries of truth, by the light of your struggles ye destroyed the grievous darkness of godlessness; and by the rays of miracles, O Martyrs...

...ye dispel the mist of passions.[^7093]

*Theotokion:* Of old [the Prophet] foresaw thee, O Maiden, as a seven-branched lampstand bearing the fire of the knowledge of God, shining upon those who are in distress in the darkness of ignorance; therefore I cry unto thee, O All-Immaculate One: Enlighten me, I pray.[^7094]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* I heard report of Thee and was afraid; I understood Thy works and was in dread. Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^7095]

*Troparion 1:* Heal my heart wounded upon the robbers' paths, I pray thee, O Forerunner, by the effective medicine of thy divine prayers.[^7096]

*Troparion 2:* Cast down, O Forerunner, the sin that yet lives in my soul; and grant revival now unto me who slip towards pleasures.[^7097]

*Troparion 3:* Be shown forth as a haven unto us who are tempest-tossed in the deep of the storm of life; bring all unto calm, O Exceedingly Rich One.[^7098]

*Theotokion:* Condemn me not, O Lord, according to my deeds, but show Thyself merciful unto me, O All-Merciful One: She that bare Thee entreats Thee together with the Baptist.[^7099]

*Tone 6. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early from the night, O Lover of mankind, enlighten me, I pray, and guide me in Thy commandments, and teach me, O Savior, to do Thy will.[^7100]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, I am he that hid Thy talent, O Christ, Thy slothful servant who have been made idle by the wicked deeds of passions; therefore send me not into the fire.[^7101]

*Troparion 2:* Having been Thy son by grace, O compassionate Christ, I became enslaved unto the enemy, and removed myself from Thee by living prodigally; therefore turn me back and save me.[^7102]

*Martyrikon:* The Martyrs stood before the judgment seat, bearing about the shield of faith; therefore with deceitful arrows the fierce apostate was unable to wound them.[^7103]

*Martyrikon:* Being put to death in body, the Martyrs put to death all the wiles of the enemy, and passed over in joy unto life that grows not old, having received the crowns of victory.[^7104]

*Theotokion:* O impassable gate! Open unto me, I pray, the gates of true repentance; and show me the path of repentance, O Pure One, guide of all.[^7105]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* Early I rise unto Thee, O Word of God, Who for mercy's sake didst unchangeably empty Thyself, and without passion didst stoop down even unto sufferings: grant peace unto me who have fallen, O Lover of mankind.[^7106]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast a temple of the Holy Trinity, and we who are gathered in this holy temple of thine, with fervent faith entreat thee, O Forerunner: Deliver us from temptations and afflictions, O all-praised one.[^7107]

*Troparion 2:* With my mind estranged from every virtue, I pray thee now, O Blessed One, who didst walk a strange path in life: Unite me unto the God of all, making me better through excellent entreaties.[^7108]

*Troparion 3:* O Prophet who didst immerse in the streams of the Jordan the abyss of tender mercy, dry up now by thy prayers the torrents of my passions, granting me fountains of tears.[^7109]

*Theotokion:* Adorned with divine rays, O Virgin, thou gavest birth unto Him Who is fair in beauty; Him do thou ever entreat, to save from corruption us who in faith and love glorify thee.[^7110]

*Tone 6. Ode 6. Heirmos:* I cried with my whole heart unto the merciful God, and He heard me from the lowest hades, and brought up my life from corruption.[^7111]

*Troparion 1:* Show me not forth as a rejoicing for demons in the dread day, O Christ Jesus, that I may not hear then the voice that sends me away into the fire of Gehenna.[^7112]

*Troparion 2:* The enemy of the righteous has drowned me in the depth of transgressions; I flee unto the deep of Thy compassions, O Jesus; guide me now unto the haven of life.[^7113]

*Martyrikon:* Having renewed the furrow of the soul with the plow of many sufferings, O Most Wise Ones, by the divine seed of faith ye manifestly bare fruit in the abundant ear of martyrdom.[^7114]

*Martyrikon:* By wounds ye wounded him that wounded you, O pious warriors; therefore, having passed over unto life, ye now heal human passions.[^7115]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as a temple unto God, O All-Immaculate One, in which He holy dwelt, deifying human nature, having made the faithful temples unto Himself.[^7116]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss of sins has compassed me about, and enduring no longer its surging, as Jonah unto the Master I cry unto Thee...

...Lead me up from corruption.[^7117]

*Troparion 1:* A branch from the root hast thou sprouted forth, O Prophet, and hearts barren of all good discernment hast thou shown fruitful in praise unto God.[^7118]

*Troparion 2:* Swiftly crush the crafty one beneath our feet, and direct our spiritual steps into the path of peace, through thine intercessions, O Forerunner.[^7119]

*Troparion 3:* Encompass thy flock with righteousness, O Prophet, delivering us from every demonic assault, and from eternal torment.[^7120]

*Theotokion:* A song of thanksgiving we offer unto thee now, O Virgin; through thee, O Pure One, having been saved from the ancient curse, we bear fruit in every blessing.[^7121]

*Tone 6. Ode 7. Heirmos:* We have sinned, we have transgressed, we have done unrighteously before Thee, neither kept nor done as Thou hast commanded us; but give us not up utterly, O God of the fathers.[^7122]

*Troparion 1:* I look upon Thy measureless mercy, who have immeasurably sinned; I know Thy tender compassion; I know Thy longsuffering and meekness. Grant me fruits of repentance, O compassionate Christ, and save me.[^7123]

*Troparion 2:* Heal, O Compassionate One, the incurable passions of my heart; grant the cutting off of my debts; lighten the heavy burden: that in compunction I may ever glorify Thee, the God of the fathers.[^7124]

*Martyrikon:* Wounded, cut to pieces, consumed by material fire, given unto lions for food, and stretched upon wheels, they denied Thee not, our living God, the divine and sacred sufferers.[^7125]

*Martyrikon:* Ye were separated from the body, but were shown not separated from God, O Martyrs, for the sake of the divine union of Him Who united Himself incorruptibly unto us. Him do ye ever entreat, that we may be delivered from every necessity.[^7126]

*Theotokion:* Without seed didst thou sprout forth Him Whom the Father incorruptibly begat; thou remainedst a Virgin after childbearing, even as before childbearing. Therefore art thou called blessed, and unceasingly glorified, O All-Pure One, as being the Mother of God.[^7127]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* The Children in Babylon feared not the flame of the furnace, but cast into the midst of the flame...

...being bedewed they sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^7128]

*Troparion 1:* Being the voice of the Word, receive now our voices, O Baptist, and deliver thy people from passions, tribulations, many distresses, and eternal torment.[^7129]

*Troparion 2:* With thy hand thou pointest out, O Prophet, the Lamb of God Who takes away the sins of the world; Him do thou ever entreat, to take away my fierce transgressions held fast through the years, and to vouchsafe me life.[^7130]

*Troparion 3:* O my soul, make haste and cry aloud, forsaking the darkening of irrational deeds: Have compassion upon me, O Jesus, by the supplications of the Baptist, and snatch me from the mire of my deeds.[^7131]

*Theotokion:* Him before Whom the heavenly ranks stand with trembling, hast thou brought forth, O Pure One, Who for goodness' sake united Himself unto men; Him do thou fervently entreat, to have compassion upon thy servants.[^7132]

*Tone 6. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Whom the heavenly hosts glorify, and before Whom the Cherubim and Seraphim tremble: let every breath and creation sing, bless, and supremely exalt unto all ages.[^7133]

*Troparion 1:* I look upon the great mercy of Thy compassions, O Christ, having sinned more than David; and I cry with him: O only Merciful One, have mercy upon me swiftly.[^7134]

*Troparion 2:* I pray that I may have a good end, yet I strive not ever to put an end to wicked deeds, having a heart hardened by darkening; have compassion upon me, O Word of God.[^7135]

*Martyrikon:* As chosen and blameless sacrifices, ye willingly offered yourselves unto the Only-begotten Word Who was sacrificed for our sake, bringing to nought all demonic sacrifices, O passion-bearers.[^7136]

*Martyrikon:* The severing of sinews, the pulling out of teeth, the cutting off of hands, the shattering of limbs, and every other torment did ye valiantly endure, O Martyrs, singing unto Christ the only Arbiter of the contest.[^7137]

*Theotokion:* A new Babe hast thou brought forth for us, Him Who is Ancient of Days, Who shows new paths upon earth and renews nature that had grown old, O unwedded, blessed Maiden.[^7138]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* In Babylon the blessed youths, imperiled for the ancestral laws...

...spat upon the senseless decree of him that reigned; and joined together, not consumed by the fire, they sang a worthy song unto the Sovereign Ruler: Sing, ye works, unto the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7139]

*Troparion 1:* Above the Word being baptized, thou didst behold the Holy Spirit descending in the likeness of a dove, and wast vouchsafed to hear the Father's voice, O Blessed One: "This is My Son Who shares My throne," Whom all creation sings: Sing, ye works, unto the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7140]

*Troparion 2:* By the fire of thy prayers, O Prophet, consume the brushwood passions of my mind, and rekindle the extinguished lamp of my heart, that looking clearly I may sing unto Him Who created the light of commandments: Sing, ye works, unto the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7141]

*Troparion 3:* I am that wretched servant who hid the talent which I received from Thee for trading; and what shall I do when Thou comest to judgment, demanding an account of everyone's work? But spare me by the supplications of Thy Forerunner, and send me not into the fire who cry aloud: Sing, ye works, unto the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^7142]

*Theotokion:* Preserve my mind in humility, O Maiden full of God's grace, who by thy childbearing didst crush demonic arrogance; and raise me up from the dunghill of passions, and satisfy me who hunger for thy grace, singing: Sing, ye works, unto the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7143]

*Canticle of the Theotokos: My soul doth magnify the Lord.*
*Tone 6. Ode 9. Heirmos:* The birth from a seedless conception is ineffable, the fruit of an unwedded Mother is incorrupt; for the birth of God renews nature. Therefore all generations rightly magnify thee as the Mother unwedded of God.[^7144]

*Troparion 1:* O Jesus Lover of mankind, Who alone knowest our weakness, for Thou didst clothe Thyself with it for mercy's sake, desiring to cleanse it: therefore cleanse the wicked defilements and suppurations of my evils, and save me.[^7145]

*Troparion 2:* Like the harlot I offer tears unto Thee, O Lover of mankind; like the publican groaning I call unto Thee: Cleanse and save me! Like the Canaanite woman I cry: Have mercy on me! Like Peter who repented, vouchsafe me forgiveness.[^7146]

*Martyrikon:* Grappling in the flesh with the bodiless ruler of this world, by the struggles of your sufferings ye cast him down, and worthily received the crowns of victory; therefore pray fervently for...

...all who in faith praise you, O Saints.[^7147]

*Martyrikon:* Thou hast sanctified the earth, O Lord, with the holy blood of Thy Saints, and their spirits, O All-Holy One, hast Thou joined unto the spiritual ranks of the holy Powers; and by them Thou dost ever sanctify those who in true faith sanctify Thee.[^7148]

*Theotokion:* We offer the Archangel's voice unto thee, O All-Pure, Blessed One: Rejoice, thou that didst contain the uncontainable God; rejoice, loosening of the curse, and bringing in of blessing; rejoice, who alone didst open the door of Paradise.[^7149]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Heirmos:* Come, ye faithful, with lofty minds let us enjoy the Master's hospitality and the immortal table in the upper place, having learned the word of Him Who ascended from the Word, Whom we magnify.[^7150]

*Troparion 1:* Heal my mind grievously wounded by the love of worldly pleasures, and calm the storm that exceedingly troubles me, and show me the upright paths of repentance, O Forerunner of the Lord.[^7151]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast seen standing in the midst of the Old and New: causing the one, O Prophet, to cease, and revealing the light of the other; in which guide us to walk diligently, with a divine conscience, that we may be delivered from eternal darkness.[^7152]

*Troparion 3:* Thou hast been joined unto the heavenly hosts, O Divinely Wise One; with them entreat Christ to save us who are upon earth, who honor thee in this thine honorable temple, O Baptist John, Forerunner of the Lord.[^7153]

*Troparion 4:* A day full of wrath, a day of darkness is the dread judgment unto them that have dark deeds; O Baptist and Forerunner of Christ, by thy prayers deliver from all condemnation then those who honor thee.[^7154]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth holier than the Cherubim, O Virgin, as having given birth unto the all-holy God. Sanctify all of us who in holy voices with faith sanctify thee by night and by day.[^7155]

*It is truly meet: Little Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^7156]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:* Having found me stripped of virtues, the enemy wounded me with the arrow of sin; but do Thou, as Physician of souls and bodies, heal the wounds of my soul, and have mercy upon me.[^7157]

*On Tuesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: ... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^7158]

*Sticheron 2:* Heal the sores of my heart, grown great from many transgressions, O Savior, as Physician of souls and bodies, granting forgiveness of transgressions ever unto those who ask; grant me tears of repentance, giving me release from debts, O Lord, and have mercy on me.[^7159]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.*[^7160]

*Martyrikon:* O Lord, if we had not Thy Saints as intercessors, and Thy goodness having mercy upon us, how should we dare, O Savior, to hymn Thee Whom the Angels ceaselessly glorify? O Knower of hearts, spare our souls.[^7161]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Of great gifts hast thou been accounted worthy, O pure Virgin Mother of God: for in the flesh thou didst bring forth One of the Trinity, Christ the Giver of life, unto the salvation of our souls.[^7162]

*It is good to give thanks unto the Lord: Trisagion, and after Our Father: troparion, litany. Then the First Hour.*[^7163]

*On Tuesday, the Beatitudes.*
*Tone 6.*

Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom, and save me, as Thou alone art the Lover of mankind.[^7164]

*Troparion 1:* O Christ Who didst accept the weeping of Peter, accept also my repentance, and grant me forgiveness of transgressions.[^7165]

*Troparion 2:* O Baptist and Forerunner of the Lord, who didst proclaim repentance unto men, pray that I may repent from the soul.[^7166]

*Troparion 3:* Having endured the surging waves of sufferings, O passion-bearers, ye now heal the infirmities of men; therefore are ye called blessed.[^7167]

*Glory...* O God glorified by the prophets in Trinity, simple and unconfused, by the supplications of the Forerunner, save me.[^7168]

*Both now...* O mighty defense of those in tribulations: stand by me who partake of worldly passions, and save me, I pray.[^7169]

*On Tuesday Evening, Tone 6.*

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross

*On Tuesday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having risen on the third day" (Тридне́внѡ воскре́слъ є҆сѝ):*[^7170]

*Sticheron 1:* When Thou wast crucified, O long-suffering Lord, Thou didst shake the whole earth, but didst establish the hearts of the faithful; therefore we sing Thy praise, and with love worship Thine unsearchable power.[^7171]

*Sticheron 2:* Thou wast struck upon the cheek, O Savior, and spat upon; Thou didst strike down the malice of the venomous foe, taking away the fall which Adam brought about, having been stolen away because of seeing.[^7172]

*Sticheron 3:* The sun was darkened, and all the earth shook, when they saw Thee, O Savior, hanging unjustly upon the Tree, and forsaking desires.[^7173]

*Then the Stichera from the Menaion for the Saint, if there be any. If not, these Stichera: Tone and podoben the same.*[^7174]

*Sticheron 4:* Standing once by the Cross at the time of the Crucifixion, the Virgin, with the virgin disciple, weeping, cried aloud: Woe is me! How dost Thou suffer, O Christ, Who art the dispassion of all?[^7175]

*Sticheron 5:* Meditating upon Thy seedless conception and all-pure birthgiving, I marvel greatly: how hast Thou thus consented to die like an evildoer, O Savior? cried the All-Pure.[^7176]

*Sticheron 6:* I am indeed crucified upon the Tree and nailed as man, and I am laid in the tomb as dead, O pure Virgin Mother! Yet as God in glory I shall rise again on the third day.[^7177]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding the most lawless people unjustly nailing Thee upon the Tree, O Savior, the Virgin and Thy pure Mother was wounded in her inward parts, even as Simeon foretold.[^7178]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:* Thy Cross, O Lord, is life and defense unto Thy people; and hoping thereon, we hymn Thee our God Who wast crucified in the flesh: have mercy on us.[^7179]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: .... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^7180]

*Sticheron 2:* Thy Cross, O Lord, opened Paradise unto mankind, and delivered from corruption, we hymn Thee our God Who wast crucified...[^7181]

*On Tuesday Evening, Tone 6.*

...in the flesh: have mercy on us.

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: .... For our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^7182]

*Martyrikon:* They that suffered for Thy sake, O Christ, endured many torments, and received perfect crowns in the heavens: let them pray for our souls.[^7183]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding our Life hanging upon the Tree, the all-blameless Theotokos cried out weeping with a mother's grief: O my Son and my God, save those who praise Thee with love.[^7184]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion, and after Our Father: troparion, litany, and dismissal.*[^7185]

*On Tuesday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 6.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* When Israel traversed the deep on foot as on dry land, and saw Pharaoh the pursuer drowning, they cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^7186]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^7187]

*Troparion 1:* I bewail every aspect of my defiled life, and the multitude of my measureless evils; what then shall I confess unto thee, O Pure One? I am at a loss and terrified; but help me, O Sovereign Lady.[^7188]

*Troparion 2:* Whence shall I begin to tell of my deceitful and grievous falls, passionate as I am? Woe is me, what shall become of me hereafter? But O Sovereign Lady, before the end have compassion upon me.[^7189]

*Glory...* The hour of death and the dread tribunal do I ever ponder, O All-Pure, yet being convicted I am all-grievously deceived by evil; but help me.[^7190]

*Both now...* The corrupter of good things, beholding me now stripped of divine virtues, and having fallen away far and become alienated from God, strives to swallow me up; O Sovereign Lady, go before and anticipate him.[^7191]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^7192]

*Troparion 1:* Passionate as I am, O Lady Mother of God, I have defiled my soul foully with measureless evils; and whither hereafter shall I go, being wholly possessed by despair?[^7193]

*Troparion 2:* And that which was made according to the image have I, prodigal one, defiled, woe is me, O Pure One, with high-minded thought;...[^7194]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 6.*

...for in likeness, both by deed, and by word, and by thought, have I committed unseemly things.

*Glory...* There is no other among men that has committed improper things, nor was any born in the world as I, O Good One, darkened in mind; for I have defiled divine baptism.[^7195]

*Both now...* I have reached the end of evils, O most holy Virgin: do thou help me quickly; heaven and earth cry bitterly because of unseemly, improper deeds.[^7196]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church godly sings, crying aloud from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^7197]

*Troparion 1:* The angelic ranks and hosts of the heavenly powers are terrified at the dominion of thy Son, O Pure One; but now having despaired, I am possessed by fearlessness.[^7198]

*Troparion 2:* All the earth was amazed and terrified, seeing me doing grievous and evil and unseemly things, and it marvels at the great tender mercy of thy Son.[^7199]

*Glory...* The bodily church have I evilly defiled, and the church of the Lord, into which men enter trembling; but I, prodigal, enter shamelessly; woe is me![^7200]

*Both now...* Show me not, O Sovereign Lady, show me not as a stranger, alienated from the protection of thy Son, being wholly unworthy; but wash me from the defilement of my transgressions.[^7201]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, illumine, I pray, the souls of those who yearn early for Thee with love: that they may know Thee, O Word of God, the true God, calling them from the gloom of sin.[^7202]

*Troparion 1:* With thy divine radiance, O Good One, heal my soul's passions, which the corrupter sowed in me; and deliver me from this bitter captivity; for he laughs, seeing me without protection.[^7203]

*Troparion 2:* Adam indeed transgressed one single commandment of thy Son, O Virgin, and fell into exile; how then shall I bewail the abyss of my transgressions, I who am a transgressor and an apostate from Him?[^7204]

*Glory...* A murderer indeed of old was Cain shown forth, and a fratricide, and he was cursed by God. What then shall I do, all-daring that I am? Having now slain my soul, and I am not ashamed.[^7205]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 6.*

*Both now...* I have rivaled cruel Esau, wholly in gluttony and surfeit; I have defiled my soul with drunkenness, and my life with self-indulgence; and who does not weep for me, passionate that I am? Woe is me![^7206]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven, I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One.[^7207]

*Troparion 1:* My manner of life is prodigal, my soul is defiled, and my whole life is miserable, and all my body have I grievously defiled in evil things; therefore make haste, O Virgin, to help me.[^7208]

*Troparion 2:* The end stands before me, and I endure not good things; conscience convicts me: for deceitful deeds and a prodigal life confront me, and I am terrified of the judgment seat of thy Son, O Pure One.[^7209]

*Glory...* A fiery river, dread and unquenchable, truly awaits the burning of my flesh, and the undying worm; but quench these for me by thy prayers, O All-Pure.[^7210]

*Both now...* Now am I seized with trembling, O Good One, and I am terrified of the snare of the deceitful one; for before the end the corrupter desires to slay me, holding me wholly as a captive and stripped of virtues.[^7211]

*Then: Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory... Both now...*[^7212]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*
Hope and wall and haven art thou unto thy people, O Virgin: for from thee without passion was born the Savior of all, and He saved those that hope on thee. Whom thou didst bewail, Thine own Son at the Cross, Him do thou also now entreat: to deliver from corruption all who hymn thee.[^7213]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Angel made the furnace dewy for the holy youths, but the command of God, consuming the Chaldeans, persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^7214]

*Troparion 1:* Sevenfold did the deceitful one kindle the flame for me with passions, and with fornications he ever slays my heart; therefore with the streams of my tears quench it, O Mother of God, and save me.[^7215]

*Troparion 2:* Defiled with the mire of my transgressions, cast me not away, O good Sovereign Lady: for seeing me in despair, the most deceitful enemy laughs; but do thou thyself...[^7216]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 6.*

...with thy mighty hand raise me up.

*Glory...* Dread indeed is the tribunal, O my passionate and senseless soul, and the torment is unending and terrible: but nevertheless now fall down before the Mother of thy Judge and God, and despair not of thyself.[^7217]

*Both now...* Darkened am I, passionate that I am, by the multitude of measureless evils, and I have defiled my soul and body and mind; therefore, O All-Pure, by the light of thy radiance quickly lead me into the dispassion of sweetness.[^7218]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and didst consume the sacrifice of the righteous one and the water: for Thou, O Christ, dost do all things merely by willing them; Thee do we supremely exalt unto all ages.[^7219]

*Troparion 1:* Thou that didst bring forth One of the Trinity, God, and didst bear Him in thine arms, O Virgin Mother, quench the fire-kindled furnace of passions, and with streams of tears wash my soul.[^7220]

*Troparion 2:* I am terrified of the coming of death, O All-Pure, and of that judgment am I now wholly afraid: yet of evil deeds am I in no wise ashamed. Have compassion upon me before the end, by thy prayers, O Virgin.[^7221]

*Glory...* Unceasing groanings grant me, O Sovereign Lady, and give showers of tears, that I may wash away my many transgressions and incurable wounds, that I may obtain eternal life.[^7222]

*Both now...* The multitude of my evils have I confessed unto thee, O Sovereign Lady, for none other in the world has so angered God, thy Son and Lord: change Him quickly unto mercy toward me, O Virgin.[^7223]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure, the Word Incarnate appeared unto men; Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^7224]

*Troparion 1:* Lo, I come unto thee, O All-Pure, with great fear and love, knowing the might of thy manifold prayer, I thy servant: for a mother's prayer unto the Son can truly do much, O Sovereign Lady; for He is inclined by tender mercy.[^7225]

*Troparion 2:* Receive the choirs of Archangels, and the multitude of the highest hosts of my Creator, of the Apostles...[^7226]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 6.*

...and assemblies of Prophets, and martyrs, and righteous, and venerable ones, and hieromartyrs: and make prayer, O Pure One, for us unto God.

*Glory...* Both now and then may I find thy help, O Pure One, in the hour wherein my spirit shall depart; quickly rescuing me, deliver me from the tormenting of demons, O All-Blameless One: and leave me not, O Good One, to be delivered up unto them.[^7227]

*Both now...* I await the compassionate Judge, even Thine own Son, the Lover of mankind, O Pure One! Despise me not, but make Him propitious unto me, that He may place me on the right hand then, O All-Blameless One, before His all-pure judgment seat; for on thee have I set my hope.[^7228]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion; and after Our Father: Troparion and the rest as usual.*[^7229]

*On Wednesday Morning.*
*After the First Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thy Cross, O Lord, is sanctified; for thereby healings come unto those who are infirm with sins. Therefore we fall down before Thee: have mercy on us.[^7230]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship at the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^7231]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Only was the wood of Thy Cross set up, O Christ, and the foundations of death were shaken, O Lord: for him whom hades had swallowed with desire, it released trembling; Thou hast shown us Thy salvation, O Holy One, and we glorify Thee, O Son of God: have mercy on us.[^7232]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "O hope of the world" (Наде́жда мíра):*[^7233]
Seeing God hanging dead upon the Tree, Whom in the flesh she bare, the Virgin weeping cried aloud: At Thine ineffable long-suffering, O Son, I marvel. How did the lawless nail Thee to the Cross as one condemned, Thee the righteous Judge and only Sovereign Master?

*After the Second Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Today the prophetic word is fulfilled: for behold, we worship in the place where Thy feet stood, O Lord; and having tasted of the tree of salvation, we have obtained freedom from sinful passions, by the prayers of the Theotokos, O only Lover of mankind.[^7234]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He has wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^7235]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord, the Jews condemned Thee unto death, the Life of all; they that passed through the Red Sea by the rod nailed Thee to the Cross; and they that sucked honey from the rock brought gall unto Thee. But of Thine own will Thou didst endure, that Thou mightest free us from the bondage of the enemy, O Christ our God: glory to Thee.[^7236]

*Martyrikon:*
*Marvelous is God in His Saints, the God of Israel.*[^7237]
The struggles of the sufferers before the tribunal, the wounds of the tyrants upon the martyrs: and the choirs of the bodiless stood by, holding the prizes of victory; the All-Wise confounded the tyrants and kings; they cast down the transgressor by the confession of Christ. O Lord Who didst strengthen them: glory to Thee.[^7238]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Standing by the Cross, the unwedded Mother cried aloud unto Him Who was born of her without seed: A sword has pierced my heart, O Son! unable to bear seeing Thee hanging upon the Tree, before Whom all creation trembles, as Creator and God. O long-suffering Lord, glory to Thee.[^7239]

*After the Third Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Всѐ ѿло́жше):*[^7240]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* When creation saw Thee willingly nailed to the Cross for our sake, O Christ, it was shaken with fear; the sun wholly darkened its light; the rocks were rent, and the divine veil of the temple was torn in twain, for the conviction of the fierce and lawless Hebrews.

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The tree brought corruption in Eden unto the first father; but the tree of the Cross blossomed forth life upon the Place of the Skull: for the malice of the enemy was trampled down, and Adam obtained mercy by the nailing of Christ, and found Paradise, crying aloud: O blessed tree![^7241]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Seeing Thee, O Christ, lifted up upon the Cross for our sake, the Ever-Virgin Maiden who gave Thee birth wounded her heart and soul with the sword of grief, and wept, lamenting with a mother's sorrow. By her supplications, have mercy on us.[^7242]

*Canon of the Precious Cross.*
*(Whose Acrostic is: Thou savest me, O Savior, nailed upon the Wood.) The work of Joseph.*
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* When Israel traversed the deep on foot as on dry land, and saw Pharaoh the pursuer...[^7243]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

...drowning, they cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^7244]

*Troparion 1:* Having stretched out Thy palms upon the Cross, O Master, Thou didst embrace cast-out men, and didst bring them unto Thine own Father, as His beloved Son and consubstantial with Him.[^7245]

*Troparion 2:* As a lamb wast Thou lifted up upon the Cross, O Word, seeking Thine own strayed sheep; and having found it, Thou didst unite it unto those that had not strayed, O Jesus. Glory to Thy dominion.[^7246]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Desiring the life on high, the gloriously renowned passion-bearing Martyrs put themselves to death on earth, having endured many torments and diverse afflictions, O richly blessed ones.[^7247]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Standing before unjust tribunals for Christ Who is righteous in all things, O Sufferers, ye endured every unjust judgment, which justifies you in God.[^7248]

*Theotokion:*
That Thou mightest mediate dispassion for all born of Adam, O Master, Thou didst endure passion upon the Cross; and seeing Thee, the earth shook, and the Sovereign Lady weeping cried aloud with a mother's grief.[^7249]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos the same.*[^7250]

*Troparion 1:* Thee, O all-holy Virgin, the Lord Who stretched out heaven and founded the earth, having come forth from thee with flesh, has shown forth unto us as an earthly heaven.[^7251]

*Troparion 2:* O thou who didst conceive God Who became man for our sake: entreat Him, O Pure One, in the day of judgment to have compassion upon us who have sinned greatly against Him.[^7252]

*Troparion 3:* The Sun having shone forth from thy holy womb with most radiant beams illuminates all the earth, O Sovereign Lady; therefore, being enlightened, we honor thee as Mother of God.[^7253]

*Troparion 4:* Dispel the gloom of improper thoughts that darken my soul, through trials, O Lady Theotokos, by the light of the holy commandments of Him Who was incarnate from thee.[^7254]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^7255]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Troparion 1:* The Cross was set up upon earth, and deception fell down; and creation was shaken, and hearts that were shaken by the enemy's assaults received confirmation through faith.[^7256]

*Troparion 2:* Raving in madness, the unjust people condemned Thee, the only Righteous One, unto death, Who dost justify earthly men and rescue them from the unjust hand of the deceiver.[^7257]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* The deceitful one stirred up all his malice, warring against the Saints; but he was vanquished, seeing those who were unyielding and partakers of the divine splendor.[^7258]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Bodily beauty was changed by severe wounds; but the spiritual brightness of Christ's passion-bearers shone forth all the more, by the operation of the Holy Spirit.[^7259]

*Theotokion:*
Fair in beauty beyond all sons of men did I know Thee to be, O Son, when I bare Thee; and how, being now crucified, hast Thou no beauty, O Christ? said the Virgin with tears.[^7260]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*

*Troparion 1:* Holding in thine arm Christ Who holds all things, deliver us from the hand of the deceitful devil and from every harm, O pure Birthgiver of God, who hymn thee.[^7261]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver us, O Virgin, from demonic disturbance, and from unjust men, and from all temptations, and from corrupting diseases, by thy prayers, O all-blameless Sovereign Lady.[^7262]

*Troparion 3:* Behold, all generations call thee blessed, O Maiden, who supernaturally didst bring forth in the flesh under time the timeless Word, and didst remain virgin again.[^7263]

*Troparion 4:* O Virgin who didst give birth unto the Lover of mankind, God, deliver me, thine unprofitable servant, in the hour of His dread coming, from every condemnation.[^7264]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church godly sings, crying aloud from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^7265]

*Troparion 1:* Seeing Thee, the unsetting Sun, O Christ, crucified upon the Tree, the sun was darkened with fear, and all creation was released from dark deception, singing Thy praise.[^7266]

*Troparion 2:* Lawless people bound Thy hands, O Savior; and all they that were bound with indissoluble bonds...[^7267]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

...were loosed, and the enemy was bound, and deception was destroyed.[^7268]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* By the stream of your blood, O Martyrs, ye drowned Pharaoh the persecutor, who with deranged mind boasted beyond measure; and rejoicing, ye passed over unto the good land.[^7269]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Having flown over the snares of the enemy on the wings of the Spirit, O Sufferers, ye ascended rejoicing whither is the primal goodness, and life, and unwaning light.[^7270]

*Theotokion:*
Thou wast wounded with the sword of the passions of Him Who was incarnate from thee, O Sovereign Lady, when thou didst see Him crucified and pierced with a lance in His all-pure ribs for our sake.[^7271]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Tone 6. Ode 4. Heirmos the same:*[^7272]
*Troparion 1:* Let us hymn the temple of God, the holy Virgin, all of us radiantly blessing her, having been deified through her and delivered from grievous evils.[^7273]

*Troparion 2:* As the door leading unto the divine entrance, as the divine paradise, as the spiritual place of sanctification, as the beauty of Jacob, we call the Virgin blessed.[^7274]

*Troparion 3:* Christ has shown thee forth as a calm haven, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, unto those who with faith and love from a pure mind invoke thee as the true Mother of God.[^7275]

*Troparion 4:* O all-pure Mary, thou wast the most pure and spacious receptacle of the divine dwelling; consume the defilement and mire of my soul.[^7276]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, illuminate the souls of those who lovingly rise early unto Thee, I pray, that they may know Thee, O Word of God, as the true God, calling us forth from the gloom of sins.[^7277]

*Troparion 1:* O Thou Who clothest heaven with clouds, O Christ, Thou wast voluntarily nailed naked upon the Cross, clothing the nakedness of my malice, and having robed me in garments of incorruption, enlightening me, O Master.[^7278]

*Troparion 2:* Christ the true Vine was crucified upon the Cross, dropping for our souls sweet new wine as truly His own, destroying all the drunkenness of the adversary's deception.[^7279]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Diversified with sacred wounds, Christ's sufferers, adorned with crowns, standing before God Who suffered in the flesh, ask remission of our transgressions.[^7280]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Looking toward the glory there, and life, and true gladness, O Passion-bearers, ye endured every tempest of grievous things, strengthened by the Passion of the Master.[^7281]

*Theotokion:*
Having contained Thee in my womb, O Christ, Who alone canst not be contained by all, I bare Thee without travails; but now I suffer travails as Thou art crucified, O Christ, the all-pure Virgin weeping said.[^7282]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* He Who created all things by His word, and by His wise providence governs all, the Lord, alone as He willed, by His tender compassion is formed from thee, O All-Pure, and ineffably becomes flesh.[^7283]

*Troparion 2:* Behold, O Virgin, prophetically didst thou conceive in thy womb God the Master and Lord over all; and ineffably, O Pure One, didst thou give birth unto Him, and after childbirth didst remain an incorrupt virgin.[^7284]

*Troparion 3:* O Mary, Sovereign Lady of all, deliver me, as thou art merciful, from dread captivity, I pray: and tear up the handwriting of my sin with the lance of Him Who was incarnate from thee.[^7285]

*Troparion 4:* The fetters of my sin tear asunder, O Virgin, by thy mediation and protection; for unto the desperate thou art hope, unto those who with faith flee unto thy divine protection.[^7286]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven, I cry aloud unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One.[^7287]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast crowned with thorns, O Long-suffering One, cutting down the thorns of passions; and pierced in Thy ribs with a lance, Thou didst put to death the many-scheming serpent who had made us dead.[^7288]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, casting down the strength of the enemy, O Master; and freeing me from bitter bondage, Thou didst receive a buffeting. I worship Thy long-suffering, O Compassionate One.[^7289]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Protected by the Cross, the sufferers tore down the wicked walls of deception, and migrated unto the city on high, adorned with crowns of victory; therefore they are called blessed.[^7290]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Having most gloriously led yourselves up unto the height of martyrdom, O Saints, ye cast down unto the earth the crafty exaltations of the enemy, and received crowns from on high.[^7291]

*Theotokion:*
How is the Radiance of the Father lifted up upon the Cross, enlightening all things, and casting down the prince of darkness? the all-pure Sovereign Lady weeping said with a mother's grief.[^7292]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Not burning thy womb, Jesus Who is fire came forth from thee in the flesh. Entreat Him, O Pure One, that those who with faith hymn thee may be delivered from fire and every torment.[^7293]

*Troparion 2:* As the comeliness of holy Angels I hymn thee, O All-Immaculate, and I pray: Consume the unseemly demonic fantasies away from me, preserving my heart in tranquility.[^7294]

*Troparion 3:* United in bodily form, He Who is Only-begotten of the Father, one of two, came forth without seed in thy womb; and He preserved thine honorable virginity unharmed, O All-Immaculate.[^7295]

*Troparion 4:* Cleanse the multitude of my sins by the multitude of thy mercy, and save me thy servant, O Good One, who flee unto thee and faithfully pray for thy mercy.[^7296]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Angel made the furnace dew-bearing for the venerable children, but the command of God burning the Chaldeans persuaded the tormentor to cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^7297]

*Troparion 1:* Being buffeted, Thou didst buffet the venomous malice of the serpent; and hanged upon the Tree, O only Mighty One, Thou didst enlighten all to cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^7298]

*Troparion 2:* Tasting of the tree, Adam the first man found death; but Christ, the second, having been put to death thereon, grants immortal life, having slain the many-scheming enemy.[^7299]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Enduring suffering, O Martyrs, by torment ye were led unto the Lord; and ye stood upon the firm rock of faith, casting down every wicked malice of the enemy by the divine nod.[^7300]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Enlightened with sufferings, O Sufferers, ye shine forth...

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

...more radiantly than the sun, and ye destroyed all the powers of darkness, singing unto Christ: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^7301]

*Theotokion:*
Thou Who art immutable in Godhead, hanging upon the Cross, didst change all creation, the Virgin said unto the Son; and beholding these things, she wept, marveling at Thy great long-suffering.[^7302]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* The Uncircumscribed sitting in the bosom of the Father now sits circumscribed, O All-Pure, in thy bosom, clothed in thy form, that He might save Adam, having become the new Adam.[^7303]

*Troparion 2:* Cease not to entreat God our Lover of mankind, O All-Immaculate, that we may receive complete forgiveness of evils, and obtain the good things prepared in the heavens for those who love Him.[^7304]

*Troparion 3:* We bless thee, O All-Immaculate, who didst bear the blessed Lord, Who crowns human nature with divine blessings, and makes new that which had grown old before.[^7305]
