*Troparion 4:* Enriched with the haven of thy salvation, O Pure One, we are saved from the tempest; and holding fast the hope that is in thee as the strength of the soul, we cry: Blessed art thou who barest God in the flesh.[^7306]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* From the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and didst consume the sacrifice of the righteous one and the water; for Thou, O Christ, dost do all things merely by willing them; Thee do we supremely exalt unto all ages.[^7307]

*Troparion 1:* The exceedingly proud serpent Thou didst humble, O transcendent God, lifted up upon the Tree in humility of heart; and Adam who was brought low by passions Thou didst lift up, O Compassionate One.[^7308]

*Troparion 2:* Changing the ancient bitterness, Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree: tasting gall, O Sweetness of all, Who art consubstantial Life, O Master, and Light, and Salvation.[^7309]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Ye bowed not the knee unto idols, O All-Praised ones! but were sacrificed as blameless lambs: and ye bent down the strength of the wicked one, hymning Christ unto the ages.[^7310]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Temples of the living Spirit were ye shown to be, O Martyrs, and ye cast down the idol temples; and ye were taken up unto the heavenly temple, hymning Christ unto the ages.[^7311]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Theotokion:*
Jacob foresaw thee, O Virgin, as a ladder leading us up unto the heavenly height, who had slipped into the depth of evils; therefore, O Pure One, we bless thee unto the ages.[^7312]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Truly we confess thee to be the Theotokos; with the Angel we faithfully cry aloud unto thee: "Rejoice!"; for thou alone didst bring forth joy for those on earth, O pure and blessed Maiden full of grace.[^7313]

*Troparion 2:* The divinely eloquent lute of thy forefather prefigured thee as an all-holy ark bearing God in the flesh, O All-Immaculate: Whom as Lord we supremely exalt unto all ages.[^7314]

*Troparion 3:* O unwedded Maiden full of God's grace, and haven for Christians in the tempest, and hope: deliver thy servants from temptations, and passions, and affliction, and from eternal fire.[^7315]

*Troparion 4:* When Thou shalt come in glory to judge all things, number me the sinner with the sheep on Thy right hand, O Master, by the supplications of Thy Mother, that I may glorify Thee unto all ages.[^7316]

*Then: My soul magnifies the Lord:*
*Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure, the Word Incarnate appeared unto men; Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^7317]

*Troparion 1:* Being Lord of times and years, in the midst of the earth at midday Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree, being crucified, O only Long-suffering One: and setting right the corrupting fruit of him who suffered a lapse in the midst of paradise.[^7318]

*Troparion 2:* The Cross was planted, and the tormentor received a mortal wound in soul, and they that were bound were loosed from corruption; and understanding was planted in all men, the enemy was brought to nought, and all things were filled with joy.[^7319]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Earth was joined with heaven, O Martyrs of the Lord, since Christ was sacrificed, being crucified; and it drew your multitude, which endured a multitude of grievous torments and shines together with the multitude of divine ministers.[^7320]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 4:* Enlightened with immaterial light, by adoption ye became gods, O Martyrs of the Lord, in the...

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

...habitations of the first-born, dwelling therein and filled with eternal glory; therefore as is due, we the faithful honor you.[^7321]

*Theotokion:*
In dread did I endure childbirth, O Master, when strangely I gave Thee birth, O Long-suffering One; beholding Whom voluntarily crucified upon the Tree, creation of old was terrified, the Immaculate One weeping cried aloud with a mother's grief; whom we magnify.[^7322]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* The Son, the unoriginate Word of God, being incarnate, becomes Son of the Virgin; and by the good pleasure of the Father and the operation of the divine Spirit, He wholly renewed my corrupted form, as Almighty.[^7323]

*Troparion 2:* O soul, arise and watch unto prayers, and unto all good things; through diligence cast away slumbering sloth, having ever a watchful guardian, the pure Mother of God, whom we magnify.[^7324]

*Troparion 3:* Hope and protection and rejoicing art thou unto the faithful, O all-immaculate Theotokos. Therefore I pray unto thy compassions: Enlighten my soul, which the darkness of many sins and evil thoughts has darkened.[^7325]

*Troparion 4:* Holy Virgin full of God's grace, open unto me the doors of light, lest the night of sin cover me; direct my life unto the calm haven of the divine commandments of Him Who became man from thee.[^7326]

*It is truly meet: litany and Exapostilarion; thereafter the usual psalms.*[^7327]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*
*Sticheron 1:* In the Cross do I have hope, O Christ, and boasting therein I cry aloud: O Lord, Lover of mankind, cast down the pride of those who confess Thee not as God and man.[^7328]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: ... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^7329]

*Sticheron 2:* Protected by the Cross, we resist the enemy, fearing not his wiles and barkings; for the proud one has been brought to nought and trampled underfoot by the power of Christ Who was nailed upon the Tree.[^7330]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us, yea, direct Thou the work of our hands.*[^7331]

*On Wednesday Morning, Tone 6.*

*Martyrikon:*
*Sticheron 3:* The memory of the Martyrs is joy unto those who fear the Lord; for having suffered for Christ's sake, they received crowns from Him, and now with boldness pray for our souls.[^7332]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Всѐ ѿло́жше):*[^7333]
When of old the undefiled Ewe-lamb and immaculate Sovereign Lady beheld her Lamb upon the tree of the Cross, she cried aloud with a mother's grief, and marveled, crying out: O sweetest Child, what is this new and most glorious sight? How has the ungrateful assembly delivered Thee up unto Pilate's judgment seat, and condemns Thee unto death, the Life of all? Yet I hymn Thine ineffable condescension, O Word.[^7334]

*It is good to give thanks: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparion, litany and the rest.*[^7335]

*On Wednesday, the Beatitudes, Tone 6:*
Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom, and save me as the only Lover of mankind.[^7336]

*Troparion 1:* Voluntarily Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree; Thou hast called back those who slipped into the depth of evils, O only Greatly Merciful One.[^7337]

*Troparion 2:* The earth shook, and the sun was extinguished, seeing Thee, the righteous Sun, voluntarily suffering upon the Tree.[^7338]

*Martyrikon:*
*Troparion 3:* Partaking of the Savior's Passions, O passion-bearers, ye also share in the divine splendor, being deified by participation.[^7339]

*Glory...* Lift me up from the depth of sin, O my Christ, Who being One of the Trinity didst voluntarily endure crucifixion.[^7340]

*Both now...* Standing by the Cross, the Virgin Mother, beholding her Son voluntarily suffering, magnified Him.[^7341]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas

*On Wednesday Evening:*[^7342]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 6:*[^7343]
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having risen on the third day" (Тридне́венъ воскре́слъ є҆сѝ):*[^7344]
*Sticheron 1:* Ministers of the great mysteries of God were ye, O theologians and God-seers, His disciples; and ye received the grace of healings, and...

*On Wednesday Evening, Tone 6.*

...heal the diseases of all men.[^7345]

*Sticheron 2:* A great refuge and protection of our souls, the driving away of evil spirits are ye, O God-seers, Apostles of the Lord; therefore we ever honor you.[^7346]

*Sticheron 3:* From every adversity, and demonic harm, and transgressions, and evil captivity deliver those who with faith praise you, O divine and blessed Apostles.[^7347]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Wonderworker Nicholas. Tone 6, and same podoben (то́йже подо́бенъ):*[^7348]
*Sticheron 4:* O blessed Nicholas, have compassion on me who fall down before thee, I pray thee, O Wise One; and enlighten the eyes of my soul, that I may purely behold the compassionate Giver of light.[^7349]

*Sticheron 5:* From the enemies that seek to afflict me deliver me, O Holy One, since thou hast boldness before God, O blessed Hierarch Nicholas; save me from harm, and deliver me from men of blood.[^7350]

*Sticheron 6:* As a stormless haven and an invincible wall have we the faithful now acquired thee, O Hierarch, and a tower of strength, and a door of repentance, and a guide of our souls, and a rampart.[^7351]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Envying, the wicked adversary ever battles against thy flock, O All-Pure, wishing to make it food for himself; but do thou, O Theotokos, deliver us from his harm.[^7352]

*O Gladsome Radiance: Prokimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord, to keep us this evening:*[^7353]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 6:*
*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, the Apostles once indeed reached the depth of the sea with nets, and once the height of the Kingdom on account of their teachings: the one eye indeed through craft examined the incomprehensible depth; the other eye through faith reached the bosom of Thine uncircumscribed being, and proclaimed unto the world Thine ageless Son. By their prayers and those of all the Saints, have mercy on us.[^7354]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: ... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion on us.*[^7355]

*Sticheron 2:* Once Thy disciples, O Christ, being tossed by the tempest in the ship, cried aloud: Teacher, save us, we perish! And now we cry aloud: O Lover of mankind, our Savior, deliver us from our distresses, we pray.[^7356]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: ... For our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^7357]

*On Wednesday at Compline, Tone 6.*

*Martyrikon:*
*Sticheron 3:* Thy Cross, O Lord, was an invincible weapon unto the Martyrs; for they saw the death set before them, and foreseeing the life to come, they were strengthened by their hope in Thee. By their prayers, have mercy on us.[^7358]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* The eye of my heart do I lift up unto thee, O Sovereign Lady; despise not my small sighing: in the hour when thy Son shall judge the world, be thou my protection and helper.[^7359]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart:*[^7360]

*On Wednesday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*(Whose Acrostic is: Most Holy, receive the lamentation of John.)*[^7361]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* He was my Helper and Protector unto salvation, this is my God, and I will glorify Him; the God of my father, and I will exalt Him: for He is gloriously glorified.[^7362]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^7363]
*Troparion 1:* How shall I approach thee, O Pure One, desperate and wretched as I am, having become wholly subject to passions through defilements? But having compassion, save my wretched soul, O all-pure Birthgiver of God.[^7364]

*Troparion 2:* Arise, O humble soul, from the depth of dishonor, and cry fervently unto the only Pure One: Have mercy on me, O all-immaculate and pure Maiden, and deliver me from passions.[^7365]

*Glory...* Drowned in sins by the tempest of temptations, stretch forth now unto me a hand of help, O Pure One, and deliver me from the dishonor of passions, that I may ever glorify thee.[^7366]

*Both now...* Deliver me from the grievous tempest of sin, O Birthgiver of God, from which I am wholly defiled. Therefore I flee unto thee: lighten now for me the burden of my transgressions.[^7367]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish upon the rock of Thy commandments, O Lord, my shaken heart, for Thou alone art holy and Lord.[^7368]

*Troparion 1:* Grown old in the dishonorable passions of sin, make me young again, O All-Immaculate, loosening the bonds of transgressions.[^7369]

*Troparion 2:* Cleanse me from clay and the mire of sin, and make me a divine temple, O Theotokos, who purely flee unto thee.[^7370]

*Glory...* Arise, O wretched soul, from the sleep of despondency; and with thine eyes look...

*On Wednesday at Compline, Tone 6.*

...up unto the only Maiden and Bride of God who saves thee.[^7371]

*Both now...* I come, O Sovereign Lady, desperate and contrite, and flee unto thy fervent prayer: save me thy servant, O Pure One.[^7372]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* The Prophet heard of Thy coming, O Lord, and was afraid, how Thou wast to be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I heard the report of Thee, and was afraid; glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^7373]

*Troparion 1:* Straightly am I to be judged for words and deeds, O Birthgiver of God, before thy Son, O All-Immaculate; may I find thee then as refuge and most mighty protection, delivering me from all torments.[^7374]

*Troparion 2:* My life is wretched, and my disposition unclean, and I have become wholly subject to passions through sins, O Pure One: cleanse me from the defilement of transgressions, and make me white as snow by thy mediation unto the compassionate God.[^7375]

*Glory...* Bound on all sides by the fetters of my sins, unable to turn unto the paths of repentance: stretch forth a hand unto me, O All-Immaculate, and make straight for me the path that guides me unto salvation.[^7376]

*Both now...* From grievous torments, and outer darkness, and Gehenna, free me by thy prayers, O Virgin: for thou hast both the will and the power, having given birth unto the only All-Good Lord.[^7377]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early from the night, O Lover of mankind, enlighten me, I pray, and guide me in Thy commandments, and teach me, O Savior, to do Thy will.[^7378]

*Troparion 1:* In the day of judgment appear, O Sovereign Lady, and stand by me, delivering me from torment and fire, that being saved I may hymn thine invincible grace.[^7379]

*Troparion 2:* Seeing that I was estranged from thy protection, O Sovereign Lady, the enemy cast my soul into the depth of transgressions, and beholding me, he laughs; but do thou help me.[^7380]

*Glory...* Cause my wretched and passionate soul to arise, O Pure One, I pray thee, O All-Immaculate: and take away from it the burden of many sins.[^7381]

*Both now...* Supplication do I offer unto thee, O All-Immaculate, from my defiled and wretched and unclean...[^7382]

...lips: have compassion on me by thy prayer, I pray thee.[^7383]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* I cried with my whole heart unto the merciful God, and He heard me from the nethermost hades, and brought up my life from corruption.[^7384]

*Troparion 1:* Receive me, the prodigal and most wretched, O Master Christ, and deliver me from torments by the supplications of Her that gave Thee birth; and deliver me from the part on the left.[^7385]

*Troparion 2:* The dark fear of hades holds me, O Pure One, and the lot of the goats troubles me; deliver me from it, O all-pure Theotokos, I pray thee.[^7386]

*Glory...* Having slain the passionate nature of my flesh, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos, make alive, O Pure One, my most wretched soul, and guide me into the upright way.[^7387]

*Both now...* A servant's supplication do I offer unto thee, senseless as I am, and flee unto thy compassionate tender mercy: turn me not away, O Pure One, put to shame.[^7388]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... both now:*[^7389]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:* Sighing from the heart and from the depths do I offer unto thee, O All-Immaculate, asking for thy beneficent protection: have mercy on my all-passionate soul, and implore the greatly merciful God to deliver me from judgment and the lake of fire, O only blessed one.[^7390]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* We have sinned, we have committed iniquity, we have dealt unjustly before Thee, neither have we kept nor done as Thou hast commanded us; but deliver us not up unto the end, O God of our fathers.[^7391]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing thee to be merciful, as the Virgin Mother loving good, all pure, I the wretched one flee unto thee: but turn me not away from thee, O All-Immaculate, put to shame.[^7392]

*Troparion 2:* Arise, O passionate soul, and stand up, O wretched one! Beat from the depth upon thy breast, and pour forth fountains of tears, that the compassionate Mother of Christ and God may have mercy on thee, wretched one.[^7393]

*Glory...* Having once been a temple of God through divine baptism, I the wretched one have wholly defiled myself, and lie prostrate terribly by a grievous fall: but do thou raise me up, O All-Immaculate, the Hope of the despairing.[^7394]

*Both now...* A heavy cloud hath covered my most wretched heart; darkness and gloom have come upon my passionate soul, O Bride of God: enlighten me with the rays of repentance, that I may glorify thee.[^7395]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Whom the heavenly hosts glorify, and Cherubim and Seraphim tremble before: sing, bless, and highly exalt Him, every breath and creation, unto all ages.[^7396]

*Troparion 1:* The river of fire troubles me, and unillumined gloom entirely shakes me; the worm terrifies me, and gnashing of teeth, O Virgin: may I then find thee a sovereign protectress.[^7397]

*Troparion 2:* Let me not be fuel for the fire, O Lord, nor let me hear the voice of Thy threat, having Thy pure Mother praying unto Thee: by Her supplications have mercy on me, I cry unto Thee.[^7398]

*Glory...* Make me to hear the divine voice, and show me, O Christ, a partaker of the station of the elect: that the place of weeping may not receive me, having the Theotokos praying unto Thee for this.[^7399]

*Both now...* Send down unto me now streams of tears, and produce in me sighing from the soul, O Pure One, ever to fall down before thy protection: that I may find release of my sins by thy prayer.[^7400]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Ineffable is the birth of the seedless conception, the incorrupt fruit of a mother that knew no man; for the birth of God renews natures. Therefore with true worship do all generations magnify thee as the Bride-Mother of God.[^7401]

*Troparion 1:* The dread day of judgment troubles me, O Virgin; gnashing of teeth consumes my heart; the station of the goats terrifies me: but may I find thee help in evils, O Theotokos, when I am to be judged.[^7402]

*Troparion 2:* Now trembling holds me, and fear of Gehenna troubles me, and what shall I do? I know not, wretched and condemned; therefore I flee unto thee, and with fervent contrition cry unto thee: despise me not, O All-Pure One.[^7403]

*Glory...* Beholding the judgment-seat, I meditate upon Thy judgment, O my Christ, wherein I am to be judged, condemned justly for my deeds; yet I have an advocate then unto Thy many compassions...[^7404]

...praying, Thy Mother, the Bride of God.[^7405]

*Both now...* Mentally touching thy feet, O All-Immaculate, I pray thee: receive my weeping, give ear unto my singing, and grant me release of transgressions, I cry unto thee, by thy prayers, O Virgin, that I may magnify thee with love.[^7406]

*It is truly meet. Trisagion; and after Our Father: Troparion and the rest as usual.*[^7407]

*On Thursday at Matins.*[^7408]

*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 6:*[^7409]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* As Thou camest into the midst of Thy disciples, O Savior, giving them Thy peace: come also unto us, and save us.[^7410]

*Verse:* Their proclamation hath gone out into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the universe.[^7411]
*Sessional Hymn 2:* The choir of Thy disciples hath enlightened the universe; for having once been fishers of fish, they became now fishers of men: for their sake, O God, have mercy on us.[^7412]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Many are the multitudes of my transgressions, O Theotokos; unto thee have I fled, O Pure One, requiring salvation: visit my ailing soul, and pray unto thy Son and our God to grant me remission of the evil things I have committed, O only blessed one.[^7413]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*[^7414]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thy disciples, O Savior, being sent unto the ends of the earth, having piously caught the nations like fish, brought them unto Thy goodness. For their sake therefore we cry unto Thee, O Christ: grant Thy great mercy unto Thy people.[^7415]

*Verse:* The heavens shall confess Thy wonders, O Lord.[^7416]
*Sessional Hymn 2:* The twelvefold company of the Apostles, O Lord, appeared twelve-rayed like the sun unto the earth; for for their sake Thou hast withered the planter of deceit; for their sake Thou enlightenest the souls of Thy servants; and for their sake save us, as Thou art tender-hearted.[^7417]

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^7418]
*Martyrikon:*
*Sessional Hymn 3:* Lord, the memory of Thy saints hath appeared today as the paradise in Eden; for in it all creation rejoices: and by their prayers Thou grantest us peace and great mercy.[^7419]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* O all-holy Theotokos, in the time of my life abandon me not; commit me not unto human protection, but do thou thyself defend and have mercy on me.[^7420]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*[^7421]
*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Всѐ ѿло́жше):*[^7422]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Into all the earth like lightning went forth the saving proclamation of Thy most wise disciples, O Master Christ, and enlightened those who were in darkness and evils, having made us sons of day and light; therefore all creation piously glorifies Thee.[^7423]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Having converted the peoples by the net of divine faith, the fishermen drew all men out of the depth of vanity, and surrendered them unto God, as they worship piously and ever magnify Him.[^7424]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* O holy Sovereign Lady, Mother of Christ our God, who ineffably didst give birth unto the Creator of all: pray ever together with the sacred Apostles unto His goodness to deliver us from passions and grant us remission of sins.[^7425]

*Canon to the holy, glorious, and all-praised Apostles. A Composition of Theophanes.*[^7426]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* As Israel traversed the deep on foot as on dry land, beholding the pursuer Pharaoh drowned, he cried: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^7427]

*Refrain: Holy Apostles, pray to God for us.*[^7428]
*Troparion 1:* Having become light unto the world, O God-proclaiming ones, enlighten us with radiant flashings, and deliver us from the night of passions and the gloom of temptations, O God-blessed ones.[^7429]

*Troparion 2:* Venerable princes over all the earth did the Spirit honorably establish you, O disciples of the Lord; therefore, having conquered the prince of the air, ye deliver the faithful.[^7430]

*Troparion 3:* Having been kindled from immaterial fire like radiant coal, burn up my material passions, O All-Wise, guiding me unto the light of dispassion and life.[^7431]

*Theotokion:* By the prayers of the Apostles, Prophets, and Martyrs, and of Her that gave Thee birth, O Pure One, Lord Jesus, King of all, have compassion on the people that have sinned against Thee.[^7432]

*Another Canon to the Holy Wonderworker Nicholas.*[^7433]
*(The Acrostic whereof is: The sixth song, O Nicholas, receive.) A Composition of Joseph.*[^7434]
*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The sensory Pharaoh was drowned with all his host; but Israel, having passed through the midst of the sea, cried: Let us sing unto the Lord our God, for He is glorified.[^7435]

*Troparion 1:* Living joyfully in the heavens, O all-wise Father, those who on earth with faith...[^7436]

...in concord hymn thee, drive away all evil sorrow of their heart by thine intercession.[^7437]

*Troparion 2:* Against harmful passions, against destructive enemies, against every malice of the deceiver, strengthen now, O Father Nicholas, those who flee with faith unto thy holy protection.[^7438]

*Troparion 3:* Wounded as we are by invisible darts, transform us by the medicine of thy prayer, O Nicholas, that we may walk soundly in the ways of the Lord.[^7439]

*Theotokion:* He Who is the co-eternal Son with the Father, desiring at the end, dwelt in thy womb for the regeneration of us the faithful, O Theotokos; therefore we sing of thee.[^7440]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^7441]

*Troparion 1:* The Divine Spirit, having refined your tongues by His awesome coming, renews broken men unto life through you, O glorious Apostles.[^7442]

*Troparion 2:* Sinning with knowledge, what mercy shall I find, O Word, in the day of judgment? Therefore I cry unto Thee: for the sake of Thy disciples have compassion on me, self-condemned.[^7443]

*Troparion 3:* Having been conformed unto the saving Passions of Christ, heal the aged and grievous passions of my soul, being physicians, O glorious Apostles.[^7444]

*Theotokion:* Having converted me who have sinned, O Christ, for the sake of the Theotokos and Thine Apostles, as God have mercy, and from eternal fire, O Savior, set me free.[^7445]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 3. Heirmos:* Having founded the thought of my soul upon the firm rock of Thy faith, establish me, O Lord: for Thee I have, O Good One, as refuge and establishment.[^7446]

*Troparion 1:* Manifestly hast thou received victories over soul-destroying passions, O all-blessed Nicholas; from which deliver us by thy prayers, who are ever evilly vanquished by them.[^7447]

*Troparion 2:* Having mortified the members of the flesh by abstinence, thou hast passed over unto the life that does not grow old: which pray that we also may receive, O Wise One, who joyfully praise thee.[^7448]

*Troparion 3:* Cease not to visit us from the heavenly height, O Hierarch, driving away all worldly distress by thy prayers unto God.[^7449]

*Theotokion:* Implore the Deliverer to grant us remission of sins, O Pure One, ever driving away all darkening from our souls by thy prayers.[^7450]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, God and Lord, the venerable Church piously sings, crying out from a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^7451]

*Troparion 1:* O calm harbors, harbors untroubled by storm, lead me unto the quiet harbor, storm-tossed as I am by the tempest of grievous transgressions.[^7452]

*Troparion 2:* Sigh, O my soul, shed tears, O my soul! Let my humble heart cleave wholly unto the Good One, that God may deliver thee from the coming flame and torment.[^7453]

*Troparion 3:* Pray unto Christ the Lord, being saviors of men, O all-blessed Apostles, that He may save us from evil temptations, as He is the Lover of mankind.[^7454]

*Theotokion:* O all-pure Virgin, O immaculate Virgin, make supplication for us with the Apostles, that we may be delivered from grievous temptations and perils.[^7455]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard, O Lord, the report of Thee and was afraid; I understood Thy works and was in dread; glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^7456]

*Troparion 1:* Heal, O holy one, my uncorrected heart wounded by the deceiver, having implored the Giver of grace, our God, O Nicholas.[^7457]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast destroyed the temples of perdition, O favorite of Christ; therefore with faith I pray thee, O Nicholas: destroy the evil idols of my mind.[^7458]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast fragrant with myrrh by righteous deeds, O God-wise one, and didst adorn the throne in Myra, O Father, Hierarch Nicholas.[^7459]

*Theotokion:* O unwedded Sovereign Lady, blessed Birthgiver of God, save those who praise thee from all harm, O only Helper of men.[^7460]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, enlighten, I pray, the souls of those who rise early unto Thee with love, to know Thee, O Word of God, the true God, calling out from the gloom of sins.[^7461]

*Troparion 1:* Having visibly walked the way that leads unto life by the most narrow passage of temptations and afflictions, O disciples of the Word, smooth the path of my soul.[^7462]

*Troparion 2:* Luminaries of the divine Orient, deliver me from the darkness of passions, and the darkening of pleasures, and tribulations, and adversities, and tempest and perils, O Apostles of Christ.[^7463]

*Troparion 3:* O God-radiant scrolls of the Word, pray that I may be written in the book of life, O Apostles, tearing up the evil bond of my sins by your prayers.[^7464]

*Theotokion:* My salvation and hope, save me who hope in thee, O unashamed hope of all, by thy supplications unto thy Son, and those of the divine disciples, O Maiden alone all-praised.[^7465]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Christ Who hast shone forth light unto the world, enlighten my heart that calls unto Thee from the night, and save me.[^7466]

*Troparion 1:* Held fast by the slumber of sloth, make me fervent by thy sacred intercessions, O Nicholas.[^7467]

*Troparion 2:* Implore Jesus the Savior, O Hierarch Nicholas, to deliver me from eternal torment.[^7468]

*Troparion 3:* Keep me from worldly scandals, and preserve me from demonic malice by thy prayers, O Hierarch Nicholas.[^7469]

*Theotokion:* He Who gives being unto us dwelt within thy womb, O Theotokos: Whom pray Thou to save us.[^7470]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the tempest of temptations, fleeing unto Thy calm haven I cry unto Thee: Bring up my life from corruption, O Greatly Merciful One.[^7471]

*Troparion 1:* As God-inscribed tablets, ye truly had in your souls the legislation of the Spirit, O glorious ones, whereby ye abolished the written law, having saved the world.[^7472]

*Troparion 2:* O soul, sinning and in mind-corrupting fashion serving evil habits, turn by conversion, and cry unto the compassionate Lord: For the sake of the Apostles, O Word, save me.[^7473]

*Troparion 3:* Unite me with the sheep on the right hand at Thine awesome and dread coming, by the prayers of Thine elect sheep, the divine...[^7474]

...preachers, O gentle Lamb Who wast slaughtered for all.[^7475]

*Theotokion:* The choir of bodiless servants prays unto Thee, O Son of God, supremely without beginning; the assembly of the disciples prays unto Thee together with Her that gave Thee birth: grant Thy mercies unto Thy people.[^7476]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Swallowed up by the whale of sin, I cry unto Thee, O Christ: as the Prophet, free me from corruption.[^7477]

*Troparion 1:* Having thee as an intellectual luminary, O Nicholas, creation is enlightened by the rays of thine innumerable miracles.[^7478]

*Troparion 2:* O unsleeping intercessor for those who flee unto thee, Nicholas, deliver me from the evil sleep of sloth.[^7479]

*Troparion 3:* Of old thou didst deliver those about to die unjustly, O Wise One; likewise also deliver me from perils and transgressions, O Nicholas.[^7480]

*Theotokion:* O only pure Virgin, who hast been favored with grace, help me, who am ever storm-tossed by worldly adversities.[^7481]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Angel made the furnace pour forth dew for the holy youths, while the command of God burning the Chaldeans persuaded the tyrant to cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^7482]

*Troparion 1:* Having drawn all men out of the depth of deceit by the hook of the word, O Apostles, ye brought them unto the Word Who was slaughtered for our sake, singing unto Him: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^7483]

*Troparion 2:* Having joined yourselves without intermediary unto Him Who appeared in the substance of flesh, O Apostles, ye were enlightened by His light-giving flashings; therefore enlighten me as I cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^7484]

*Troparion 3:* Implore the Good Shepherd, O divine disciples, to seek out me who have gone astray in the mountains of transgression, and to save him who cries fervently: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^7485]

*Theotokion:* Together with the Prophets, Apostles, and Martyrs, O Virgin, pray Thou unto Him Whom thou hast borne: to deliver me from earthly evils, and vouchsafe me, O Sovereign Lady, to be a partaker of heavenly goods.[^7486]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 7. Heirmos:* Having heard the song of Thy holy youths, and bedewed the burning furnace: blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^7487]

*Troparion 1:* By the power of God, O all-blessed one, thou didst trample the regiments of the heretics, and from their deceit hast saved thy flock, O Nicholas.[^7488]

*Troparion 2:* Having appeared unto those sailing, and having nourished thy city that was suffering, deliver me from soul-destroying famine by thy prayers, O Father Nicholas.[^7489]

*Troparion 3:* Establish by thy prayers, O Nicholas, those who call upon thee for help, O Father, calming the demonic evil tempest.[^7490]

*Theotokion:* O Sovereign Lady of all men, holy Theotokos, completely liberate me from the servitude of passions by thy prayers.[^7491]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Out of the flame Thou didst pour forth dew for the holy ones, and didst consume with fire the sacrifice of the righteous one with water: for Thou dost all things, O Christ, by Thy will alone; Thee we highly exalt unto all ages.[^7492]

*Troparion 1:* With the sickle of prayers clean away all tares of my thoughts, O divine husbandmen, Apostles of the Savior, that I may bring forth an ear of saving fruit.[^7493]

*Troparion 2:* Hearts grown cold ye renewed with the plow of the word, and having sown the honorable doctrine, ye reaped an ear of a hundredfold among those that are saved, O God-seers.[^7494]

*Troparion 3:* Having put to sleep the malice of the enemy, ye fell asleep with the sleep resembling the righteous, having become unsleeping intercessors for us, O disciples of the Word, unto all ages.[^7495]

*Theotokion:* Deliver me from the unquenchable fire, O Lord, by the prayers of Thy Prophets, and Martyrs, and Apostles, and of the Virgin who knew no man, who ineffably contained Thee in her womb, O Merciful One.[^7496]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Thy holy youths in the furnace imitated the Cherubim, singing the Thrice-Holy hymn: Bless, sing, and highly exalt Him unto all ages.[^7497]

*Troparion 1:* Having ascended unto the height of the most radiant life, thou hast appeared renowned unto the universe, O God-bearer, by diverse wonderworkings enlightening those who are in darkness, O Nicholas.[^7498]

*Troparion 2:* For those who watch in divine grace and honor thee, O Nicholas, put to sleep the malice of hostile men who assail us, O blessed one.[^7499]

*Troparion 3:* Having broken all the snares of the enemy with spiritual strength, from them deliver me by thy...[^7500]

...prayers, piloting my life, O God-wise Hierarch Nicholas.[^7501]

*Theotokion:* We hymn thee, O Virgin Birthgiver of God, for whose sake the curse became unfruitful, and blessing came unto all who know thee as the only unwedded Mother of our God.[^7502]

*Then: My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^7503]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* It is impossible for men to see God, upon Whom the ranks of Angels dare not gaze; but through thee, O All-Pure One, the Word incarnate became visible unto men, Whom magnifying with the heavenly hosts, we call thee blessed.[^7504]

*Troparion 1:* Having preached throughout all the world God Who joined Himself unto men, and inseparably deified our mortal nature by the union, ye freed all from godless deceit; therefore ye are ever called blessed, O divine Apostles.[^7505]

*Troparion 2:* Having depicted the Passion of Christ, O Apostles, by diverse passions of the body, mortify the passions of my flesh: and make alive my exhausted, suffering, and deadened soul.[^7506]

*Troparion 3:* Be Thou a way for me, guiding me unto the unerring way of Thy commandments, O Lover of mankind, who have fallen into the pit of sin, and am wandering, and have turned aside unto crooked paths, and am suffering.[^7507]

*Theotokion:* The choirs of Angels and Archangels, Authorities and Powers, Principalities, Dominions, Cherubim, and Seraphim, and Thrones, O Christ: the assembly of the Apostles, together with Her that gave Thee birth, pray unto Thee, O only King: save Thy servants.[^7508]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Having received the "Rejoice" from the Angel, and given birth unto thy Creator, O Virgin, save those who magnify thee.[^7509]

*Troparion 1:* Adornment of hierarchs, fount of miracles, Nicholas: implore the God Who loves mankind that we may find remission of sins.[^7510]

*Troparion 2:* As of old thou didst deliver the prisoners from death, so also deliver us from worldly tribulations, O Nicholas.[^7511]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast buried in Myra of Lycia, yet thou pourest forth myrrh ever, Nicholas, ever driving away the stench of passions.[^7512]

*Troparion 4:* The coming of the Creator is at the doors; be not slothful, O my soul...[^7513]

...but cry aloud: By the prayers of Nicholas, O Christ, save me.[^7514]

*Theotokion:* Having given birth unto the Light, O All-Hymned One, enlighten the blinded apple of my heart, that I who am saved through thee may hymn thee.[^7515]

*It is truly meet: litany, exapostilarion, and the usual psalms.*[^7516]

*On the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 6:*[^7517]

*Sticheron 1:* Having wisely stilled the tempest of demonic deceit in stillness, O Apostles of Christ, ye guided the whole world into the Orthodox faith; and now pray ye for our souls.[^7518]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: - - - And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^7519]

*Sticheron 2:* With songs let us worthily honor the memory of the all-wise disciples of Christ our King, O faithful; for they proclaimed the faith of the Trinity in the world.[^7520]

*Verse: And let the brightness - - - And establish Thou the works of our hands upon us, yea, the work of our hands establish Thou.*[^7521]

*Martyrikon:* Marvelous did our God make His chosen and holy ones. Rejoice and be glad, all His servants: for He hath prepared crowns and His Kingdom for you; but we entreat you: forget us not also.[^7522]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Thou art relief unto the sorrowful, transformation unto the ailing, O all-hymned Birthgiver of God: save this city and people, peace unto those who are tossed about, calm unto the shipwrecked, thou only protectress of the faithful.[^7523]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion; after Our Father: Troparia, litany, and the rest.*[^7524]

*On Thursday, at the Beatitudes.*[^7525]

*Tone 6:*

*Troparion 1 (Refrain):* Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom, and save me, as the only Lover of mankind.[^7526]

*Troparion 2:* Having gone forth like mystical rays into the world, O glorious Apostles, ye enlightened the human race with faith.[^7527]

*Troparion 3:* Like clouds ye sprinkled the water of divine understanding in the world, O enlightened Apostles of the Lord.[^7528]

*Troparion 4 (Martyrikon):* Approaching the fire of torment, ye received from God divine dew cooling you, O Martyrs.[^7529]

*Glory:* Worshipping the Holy and Honorable Trinity, O faithful, with one accord let us cry aloud: By the prayers of the Apostles, save us all.[^7530]

*Both now:* O Birthgiver of God who gavest birth unto the Word beyond understanding, co-unoriginate with the Father, pray Him that our souls may be saved.[^7531]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross

*On Thursday Evening:*[^7532]

*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*[^7533]

*Tone 6, Podoben: "The Angelic Powers" (А҆́ггелскїѧ си̑лы):*[^7534]

*Sticheron 1:* Tripped up because of the tree, the forefather falls and endures bitter condemnation; but Christ, being nailed unto the Tree, dissolved it: and He renewed him that was broken, and took him upon His shoulder, and made him co-enthroned with Himself and the Father and the Holy Spirit.[^7535]

*Sticheron 2:* Grant me the thought of compunction, O my Christ, that I may ever bear in mind and heart the honorable Passions which Thou didst endure in the flesh for our sake: receiving light from them and weeping, ever to stand before Thy Cross, and to behold Thee upon it lifted up as a Lamb.[^7536]

*Sticheron 3:* The earth was dismayed, the air grew dark, beholding God insulted by a servant, Him Who melts the mountains with a rebuke, and by His look alone thickens the deeps, upon the Tree closing His eyes, Him Who embracs all breath in His hand, being lifeless and dead in the flesh.[^7537]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^7538]

*Tone and Podoben the same.*[^7539]

*Sticheron 1:* Make alive my soul that is deadened by many passions, O Lady, who without passion gavest birth unto God the Word, Who being passionless endured passions, and put death and Hades to death: by His mortification, mortify the sin that dwells in me, and direct my deadened thoughts toward divine things.[^7540]

*Sticheron 2:* When Thou didst deign to be lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ, then seeing the disciple whom Thou lovedst standing by, and Thy Mother, Thou saidst unto them: Behold thy Mother, O Virgin; and behold thy son, O pure Virgin Mother, him whom I have loved, the virgin and My disciple.[^7541]

*Sticheron 3:* From that time forth the virgin led the Virgin into his own house; for being called a son by the all-wise Son, he showed filial devotion unto the Mother, becoming...[^7542]

...a warm adoption and kinship unto her at all times. By their prayers, O Christ, save our souls.[^7543]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* When the Ewe-lamb beheld the Lamb born of thee nailed unto the Cross, O Ewe-lamb, she cried aloud weeping: Woe is me, my sweetest Child! without travails I gave birth unto Thee before; but now my heart is pierced, and I cannot bear to see Thee crucified, O Christ, my most merciful Son.[^7544]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day.*[^7545]

*On the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*[^7546]

*Sticheron 1:* Thy Cross, O Lord, is life and protection unto Thy people; and hoping thereon, we hymn Thee our God Who wast crucified in the flesh: have mercy on us.[^7547]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes... So are our eyes toward the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^7548]

*Sticheron 2:* Thy Cross, O Lord, hath opened Paradise unto the human race; and being delivered from corruption, we hymn Thee our God Who wast crucified in the flesh: have mercy on us.[^7549]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are exceedingly filled with contempt. Yea, our soul hath been exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^7550]

*Martyrikon:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, did not deny Thee, nor did they depart from Thy commandments; by their prayers, have mercy on us.[^7551]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:*

*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having risen on the third day" (Тридне́внѡ воскре́слъ є҆сѝ):*[^7552]

Beholding Thee crucified, O Christ, She that gave Thee birth cried aloud: What is this strange mystery that I behold, O my Son? How dost Thou die upon the Tree, suspended in the flesh, O Giver of life?[^7553]

*On Thursday at Small Compline.*[^7554]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos (The acrostic whereof is: Grant me, O Virgin, tears of repentance):*[^7555]

*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having walked upon dry land with footsteps along the deep, Israel, seeing the persecutor Pharaoh drowned, cried aloud: Let us sing a song of victory unto God.[^7556]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Birthgiver of God, save us.*[^7557]

*Troparion 1:* Receive my supplication, O Virgin, from unworthy mouth, of me who have dared to bring it boldly from defiled lips: and shine the light of compunction, O Pure One, upon thy servant.[^7558]

*Troparion 2:* Grant me showers of tears by thy prayers, O Virgin Mo...[^7559]

...ther pure, that I may weep fervently over what I have committed upon earth, and by thee escape every torment.[^7560]

*Glory:* Reject not my weeping, O thou who gavest birth unto the Fount of mercy: but mercifully, O Good One, with thy compassionate eye, O Birthgiver of God, heal the passions of my soul.[^7561]

*Both now:* Weep with sighing, O my soul, and wail utterly for thyself, falling down before the Mother of God, and saying: Deliver me who am guilty from fearful torment.[^7562]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy as Thou, O Lord my God, Who hast exalted the horn of Thy faithful, O Good One, and established us upon the rock of Thy confession.[^7563]

*Troparion 1:* There is none other like unto me in sinning, O most holy Pure One, being enslaved unto the evils of measureless thoughts; but hoping in thee, I pray: snatch me from these.[^7564]

*Troparion 2:* Behold, all the malicious evils of the enemy have come upon me like wounds, and having grown heavy, have grown exceedingly foul, and lead my soul into every fall.[^7565]

*Glory:* Great is the multitude of my transgressions; therefore, O Most Holy One, falling down in faith, I, the humble one, cry unto thee: in the fearful hour, make supplication unto Him Whom thou didst incarnate.[^7566]

*Both now:* Holy Birthgiver of God, deliver me who am guilty of every condemnation, as thou hast maternal boldness toward God, and count me worthy of His bridal chamber.[^7567]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power, God and Lord, the honorable Church divinely sings, crying out with a pure mind, keeping festival in the Lord.[^7568]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me a fount of tears, O Lady, that I may wash away the defilement of my humble soul, and find the beauty which I lost by the counsel of the serpent.[^7569]

*Troparion 2:* O my merciful God, O God Who lovest mankind, Creator of mercy! pour forth Thy mercy upon me now: my hope and protectress, Thy Mother, entreats Thee.[^7570]

*Glory:* Having compassion on me, the prodigal, me the wretched, who have sinned much against thee senselessly and shamelessly, O All-Immaculate One: save me, and deliver me from Gehenna.[^7571]

*Both now:* Destroy the devouring pasture of death, O thou who gavest birth unto the Life of all; there...[^7572]

...fore I also cry unto thee: I have sinned, save me, O Pure One, by thy maternal supplications.[^7573]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* With Thy divine light, O Good One, illumine with love the souls of those who rise early unto Thee, I pray Thee, to know Thee, O Word of God, the true God, Who callest us from the darkness of sin.[^7574]

*Troparion 1:* Have mercy on me, O Pure One, who have placed all my hope in thee: and have compassion on him that calls unto thee in faith, overlooking all the cruel passions of my soul.[^7575]

*Troparion 2:* Save me from passions, sorrow, and harm of the malicious adversaries: and deliver my humble soul from their barking, lest they say, O Virgin: We have prevailed against him.[^7576]

*Glory:* Having appeared pure in body and soul, O All-Pure One, deliver my soul from defilement, granting me to live purely, and guide me to do the divine will of the Lord.[^7577]

*Both now:* I have thee alone as a refuge, and reconciliation toward God, who have squandered all my life in many transgressions; therefore, O Birthgiver of God, have compassion on me.[^7578]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life surging with the storm of temptations, having fled unto Thy calm haven, I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, O Most Merciful One.[^7579]

*Troparion 1:* Direct my slight sighing, O Lady, and the lifting up of my hands, as a fragrant sacrifice, and make me worthy to behold thy countenance with a pure conscience.[^7580]

*Troparion 2:* Despise not the supplication of my words, O good Sovereign Lady, who gavest birth unto the merciful God Who shone forth from thy womb: but grant me divine forgiveness before the end.[^7581]

*Glory:* Prodigally have I, the most wretched one, bowed down unto passions, and departed from God; pray Him to save me, O Good One, for unto thee have I fled; and adorn me with the former garments.[^7582]

*Both now:* Fiery Jesus burnt not thy womb, but comes in the flesh from thee; entreat Him, O Pure One, to deliver from fire and every torment those who hymn thee with faith.[^7583]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... both now...*[^7584]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6:*

Pure Virgin and glorious, glory of Angels! when thou didst stand...[^7585]

...by the Cross of thy Son and thy God, unable to endure the malice of enemies, thou criedst aloud, wailing with sighings, O only blessed one: How doth the Lover of mankind endure what is inflicted by all these?[^7586]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The children in Babylon feared not the flame of the furnace, but being cast into the midst of the flame, being bedewed, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^7587]

*Troparion 1:* Arise, O my soul, from the evils which thou hast committed! why sleepest thou further? why liest thou down in the sleep of despondency? Cry aloud unto the Birthgiver of God: Most Holy One, help me.[^7588]

*Troparion 2:* I revere the likeness of thy form, yet dare not gaze upon thee, O Most Holy One: I pray to receive remission of debts, and purely to behold thine all-pure icon.[^7589]

*Glory:* Unto thy protection, O Birthgiver of God, do I flee, and fall down to receive forgiveness; despise me not, O Lady, but having compassion, save me.[^7590]

*Both now:* By the manifold evils of the soul-corrupter have I defiled soul and mind, I pray thee, O All-Pure One: despise me not, and snatch thy servant from his temptations.[^7591]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* For the sake of the laws of their fathers, the blessed youths in Babylon, risking their lives, spat upon the senseless decree of the ruler; and joined together, being not burnt by the fire, they sang a worthy song unto the Almighty: Praise the Lord, all ye works, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7592]

*Troparion 1:* Having sinned beyond measure, and defiled my body in sins, senseless as I am, I reflect upon the day of unerring judgment, and in perplexity I fear, and am dismayed and tremble; therefore I fall down unto thy tender-heartedness, O Lady: cast me not away, but see my sorrow, and deliver me from every condemnation and manifold torments.[^7593]

*Troparion 2:* Having despised the divine law, I lie wholly in sloth, wretched as I am; looking down with favor, O All-Pure One, raise me up quickly, and make me obtain the portion of the saved, that rejoicing I may cry unto thee: Rejoice, joy of the world, only protectress of those who call in faith upon thy firm protection.[^7594]

*Glory:* Woe is me, O my soul! how shall we stand then in fear...[^7595]

...and trembling before the fearful judgment-seat, when the Angels shall stand in fear and trembling? Having the Birthgiver of God as a good protectress, bend thy knees, and lift up thy hands unto the heights, crying aloud: Look down and spare me then with thy merciful countenance, O Virgin.[^7596]

*Both now:* Show the unabashed might of thy protection now; for as One indebted, thy Son will be inclined by maternal supplications, O Virgin. Anticipate therefore now in the hour of need, that I go not away unprepared, but may yet live, O all-hymned, pure Birthgiver of God.[^7597]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Weep not for Me, O Mother, beholding in the tomb the Son Whom thou didst conceive in the womb without seed; for I shall arise and be glorified, and as God I shall unceasingly exalt in glory those who in faith and love magnify thee.[^7598]

*Troparion 1:* That I may obtain the glory on high, O Virgin, I pray thee: grant me relief, and deliver me from the myriad debts, overlooking all things, O All-Pure One, that were done in ignorance and with knowledge, together by day and by night: that rejoicing I may offer unto thee a thanksgiving song.[^7599]

*Troparion 2:* Snatch me from all sorrows, O Maiden, and set my feet upon the rock of divine salvation; for having now acquired thee as an unconquerable protectress, I hope to pass through the wall and barrier which I built by the disobedience of the tree.[^7600]

*Glory:* I pray thee who gavest birth unto the merciful Judge and Master: receive the boldness of defiled lips, and my poor singing: and abhor me not, who am more sinful than all men; for after God I, thy servant, have thee as protectress.[^7601]

*Both now:* As thou art higher than all creatures, O Birthgiver of God, thou didst hold in thy womb God incarnate; entreat Him fervently, O Pure One, to release thy servant from every assault, that with free voice I also may glorify thee.[^7602]

*It is truly meet: a prostration, Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia and the rest as usual.*[^7603]

*On Friday Morning:*[^7604]

*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*[^7605]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thy Cross, O Lord, is sanctified; for thereby healings come unto those who are ailing through sins. For...[^7606]

...this cause we fall down before Thee: have mercy on us.[^7607]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^7608]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* No sooner was the wood of Thy Cross set up, O Christ, than the foundations of death were shaken, O Lord: for him whom Hades swallowed with greed, it released with trembling; Thou hast shown us Thy salvation, O Holy One: and we glorify Thee, O Son of God, have mercy on us.[^7609]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* With the virgin disciple, the Virgin Mother standing by the Cross, unto Him Who proceeded from her, not enduring to see Him lifted up upon the Cross, cried aloud, overcome by wailing: How dost Thou suffer, Who art passionless in nature? I glorify Thine ineffable goodness, O my Son, which surpasses all understanding.[^7610]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*[^7611]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Today the prophetic word is fulfilled; for behold, we worship at the place where Thy feet stood, O Lord: and having tasted of the tree of salvation, we have obtained freedom from sinful passions, by the prayers of the Birthgiver of God, O only Lover of mankind.[^7612]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^7613]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord, the Jews condemned Thee unto death, the Life of all; they who passed through the Red Sea with a rod nailed Thee unto the Cross; and they who sucked honey from the rock brought Thee gall. But voluntarily didst Thou endure, that Thou mightest free us from the servitude of the enemy. O Lord, glory to Thee.[^7614]

*Martyrikon:*

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^7615]

Light is ever for the righteous; for the Saints, being enlightened by Thee, shine ever as luminaries, having extinguished the lamp of the impious; by whose prayers, O our Savior, do Thou enlighten my lamp and save me.[^7616]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Holy, all-pure Virgin, glory of Angels! when thou didst stand by the Cross of thy Son and thy God, not enduring to see the reproaches of the enemies, thou criedst aloud, wailing maternally: How dost Thou endure the threats of all, O Lover of mankind? I glorify Thy long-suffering.[^7617]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*[^7618]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Upon the Tree of the Cross, for the sake of tender mercy and compassion Thou wast lifted up; and being pierced in the side with a spear, Thou didst tear up the cruel handwriting of sin, as all-powerful God: therefore, O Word, we piously hymn Thine ineffable dispensation.[^7619]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Cross, expeller of demons, physician of the ailing, might and guardian of the faithful, victory of kings, praise of the Orthodox in truth, confirmation of the Church of Christ: be Thou our rampart and wall and guardian, O blessed Tree.[^7620]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Him Who was incarnate of thy pure blood hanging voluntarily upon the Cross, O only undefiled Ewe-lamb, Virgin Birthgiver of God, thou criedst aloud, O Maiden, with bitter tears: O my gentle Son, I hymn all Thy fearful dispensation.[^7621]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*[^7622]

*(The work of Theophanes.)*[^7623]

*Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Him Who of old hid the pursuing tyrant beneath the wave of the sea, the children of the saved hid beneath the earth; but let us, as the maidens, sing unto the Lord: for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^7624]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^7625]

*Troparion 1:* By Thy precious Passion Thou hast honored dishonored human nature; therefore, honoring Thee with fear, we magnify Thy dominion, and in faith glorify Thee: for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^7626]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy Blood, O Word, Thou hast stopped the unrighteous shedding of blood, and hast cleansed human nature from malicious defilement, O All-Powerful One: therefore, being saved, we glorify Thy dominion.[^7627]

*Martyrikon 1:* The praiseworthy resistance of the Martyrs truly conquered the bloodthirsty beast; it subdued the nature of fire with the dew of divine grace, and stilled the violent tempest of polytheism.[^7628]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having dried up the showers of delusion with the streams of your blood, ye pour forth showers of healings unto the world, and drive away the flame of the passions of godlessness; therefore, O Martyrs, ye are called blessed.[^7629]

*Theotokion:* The unwedded Maiden, beholding Thee, the adornment of the faithful, Who art fair in beauty, having neither form nor comeliness in Thy suffering, cried aloud maternally and with love glorified Thee.[^7630]

*Other Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^7631]

*Tone and Heirmos the same.*[^7632]

*Troparion 1:* Thou hast power to do whatsoever thou desirest; thou hast a Son...[^7633]

...inclined unto thy supplications; thou hast strength and dominion, an unconquerable intercession, O Lady Birthgiver of God: therefore save thy servant.[^7634]

*Troparion 2:* O Lady of the world, helper unto all, deliverance and salvation unto those on earth: deliver thou thy servants who praise thee in faith from manifold troubles and passions and distress.[^7635]

*Troparion 3:* Thou art the protectress of the world, O Virgin; thou art the boast of Christians and our hope: be thou protectress and confirmation, joy and deliverance unto my wretched soul, O Divinely-Blessed One.[^7636]

*Troparion 4:* O hope and protectress of the hopeless, shameless helper unto all: having opened unto me the heavenly doors, bring me unto thy Son, O Lady, and save me who am gone astray.[^7637]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Beholding Thee Who suspended all the earth without restraint upon the waters hanging upon the Place of the Skull, creation was shaken with great dismay, crying aloud: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord.[^7638]

*Troparion 1:* Beholding Thee Who suspended all the earth without restraint upon the waters lifted up upon the Tree and pierced in the ribs, the sun was darkened, knowing Thee, O Lord, as the Enlightenment of all.[^7639]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy nails, O Long-Suffering One, the evil-doer who of old wounded Adam in Paradise is wounded, and having been wounded, he remains incurable unto ages; while we the faithful have found healing from all wounds.[^7640]

*Martyrikon 1:* When the Angelic choir beheld the companies of martyrs being slaughtered for the sake of the Lamb Who was slain, it marveled how material men conquered immaterial enemies, receiving the crowns of victory.[^7641]

*Martyrikon 2:* Being clothed with power, O Holy Ones, in the strength of the only Almighty One, ye wrestled with the enemy, and trampled his powerless might, receiving crowns from God.[^7642]

*Theotokion:* When thou didst behold Him Who was born of thee wounded by a spear, thou wast wounded in heart, O all-pure, All-Immaculate One, and marveling, thou saidst: What hath the most lawless assembly rendered unto Thee, O Child?[^7643]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Tone and Heirmos the same.*[^7644]

*Troparion 1:* With the waters of salvation give drink, O Virgin-Mother Maiden, delivering from darkness and the unquenchable flame of fire thy supplicants, O Most Pure One, who honor thee as the Birthgiver of God and glorify thee in faith.[^7645]

*Troparion 2:* Being wholly scabbed with the wounds of transgressions, I flee unto thee, O Birth-giver of God, and pray: do thou destroy the wounds thereof and save me, granting me divine health.[^7646]

*Troparion 3:* Ruling over all creatures, O pure Birthgiver of God, show me, who am enslaved by cruel offenses and unseemly deeds, free from them by thy divine power.[^7647]

*Troparion 4:* I know and understand thy tender compassion, O most pure Birthgiver of God! Therefore I flee unto thee and pray: that I may obtain thy help, O Bride of God, so that I may find purification of my evils.[^7648]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Foreseeing Thy divine emptying upon the Cross, Habakkuk cried aloud in terror: Thou didst cut through the dominion of the mighty, O Good One, joining Thyself unto those in hades as all-powerful.[^7649]

*Troparion 1:* Having stretched forth Thy hands, O Lord, taking away the lack of restraint toward tasting of the tree, Thou wast sacrificed upon the Tree, having willed to save the dead; and from Thy ribs Thou didst drop forth forgiveness unto those described.[^7650]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst endure to suffer, O Most Good One, that Thou mightest loose us from irrational passions; Thou didst taste of gall, O Master, pouring forth divine sweetness unto me; Thou wast put to death, granting life unto me.[^7651]

*Martyrikon 1:* Bearing the fire of divine love in their rational soul, the passion-bearers of Christ quenched the flame of the transgressors and appeared as illuminating candles.[^7652]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having separated themselves from carnal friendship, the divine friends of the King of all pressed forward steadfastly unto every torment, and having conquered, were crowned with glory.[^7653]

*Theotokion:* Without passion I gave birth to Thee; and how do I now behold Thee partaking of passion? How dost Thou endure this, cried aloud the Virgin, O unoriginate Son? I glorify Thy longsuffering.[^7654]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^7655]

*Troparion 1:* Having despaired of my salvation, calling aloud I cry unto thee, and stretch forth my hands...[^7656]

...stretch forth: have mercy upon me, O Lady Mother of God, and be thou my helper and protection.[^7657]

*Troparion 2:* I am gripped by the tempests of immeasurable passions and sins; with tears I cry unto thee: O pure Lady, by thy divine guidance do thou save me.[^7658]

*Troparion 3:* Making easy for me the fearful trial at the departure of my soul and its parting from the body, O Virgin Lady: show me to be a partaker of the divine rest there.[^7659]

*Troparion 4:* Behold my affliction, behold, O Most Pure One, my sighings; behold my despair, and show forth thy great grace upon me, filling my humble soul with gladness.[^7660]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Unto Thee I rise early, O Word of God, Who for tender mercy's sake didst empty Thyself unchangeably, and dispassionately didst bow down even unto passions: grant peace unto me who am fallen, O Lover of mankind.[^7661]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast seen as dead upon the Place of the Skull, Who didst slay hades by the passion of Thy flesh; hanging there, O Christ, Thou hadst neither form nor comeliness, desiring in Thy love for mankind to adorn me.[^7662]

*Troparion 2:* Desiring to nail fast the passionate affections of the first Adam, Thou wast nailed fast with nails, and being pierced with a spear, Thou dost forbid the fiery sword, O Christ, to bar the entrance unto Thy servants.[^7663]

*Martyrikon 1:* Following the passions of the Master, the choirs of martyrs were nailed unto the tree, and were deprived of both hands and feet, being directed divinely unto the path of honorable martyrdom.[^7664]

*Martyrikon 2:* With a perfect mind, O martyrs of Christ, ye utterly cast down the proud mind that exalts itself in audacity, having humbled yourselves in imitation of Him Who exalted all through humility of heart.[^7665]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst sprout forth from the root of Jesse, O Virgin, and as a flower didst bring forth the Planter of the world, Who dried up the sprouts of godlessness by the divine plant of the Cross, which He received in the flesh.[^7666]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^7667]

*Troparion 1:* Piously glorifying thee as the true Birthgiver of God, O Pure One, and kissing thine...[^7668]

...icon, through thy supplications unto thy Son we indeed enjoy enlightenment and unceasing joy with all the saints, being delivered from fire and torment.[^7669]

*Troparion 2:* According to the multitude of thy mercies and tender compassion, O Good One, unto me who flee unto thee in faith and ask thy prayers, grant purification of sins and pardon of offenses, O Birthgiver of God, Ever-Virgin.[^7670]

*Troparion 3:* From cruel despair, brought on by frequent tribulations of life, passions, sorrows, and demonic attacks, deliver me, O good and most pure Virgin; and grant me the comfort and enlightenment of salvation.[^7671]

*Troparion 4:* Having spent my life in slothfulness, I anger thee, O Virgin; I, the wretched one, am ever in despondency, and neglect thy mercy. Yet I pray thee, O Good One: permit me not to become a cause of rejoicing for demons, and food for Gehenna.[^7672]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah was caught, but not held fast in the belly of the sea monster; for bearing the image of Thee Who didst suffer and wast given over unto burial, he came forth from the beast as from a bridal chamber; and he cried unto the guard: Ye who observe vain and false things have forsaken this mercy.[^7673]

*Troparion 1:* Crossing his hands of old, Jacob prefigured the Cross when blessing the children; therefore, O Most High, ascending thereon, Thou dost loose mankind from the ancient curse, and dost now pour forth blessing unto those who bless Thee, O only Blessed and Most Good Creator.[^7674]

*Troparion 2:* Prefiguring Thy passion, O Word, the great Moses of old lifted up the brazen serpent upon the tree, snatching those who looked upon it from the venomous biting of serpents; for when Thou, O Master, wast crucified, we all the faithful were delivered from the injury of the God-detested serpent.[^7675]

*Martyrikon 1:* As though another were suffering, the divine and honorable sufferers of old endured their suffering, foreseeing eternal recompense and rejoicing. By their prayers, O merciful Christ, deliver those who hymn Thee from temptations, sins, and adversities.[^7676]

*Martyrikon 2:* By the streams of their blood, the honorable and divine passion-bearers stopped the torrents of delusion, and truly appeared as fountains...[^7677]

...pouring forth the water of the knowledge of God unto the faithful. By their prayers, O Savior of the world, shower down forgiveness unto all, both life and purification of sins, and great mercy.[^7678]

*Theotokion:* Beholding the Chief Shepherd and Master lifted up upon the Tree, the Ewe-lamb wept and cried aloud maternally: What is this new mystery, O Child? How is it that, being immortal by essence, Thou hast received death, desiring to deliver mankind from corruption?[^7679]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^7680]

*Troparion 1:* I fall down before thee and pray, O pure Lady of the world! Destroy the passions of my soul, the cruel desires of the flesh, and the defeats of my mind; and cast down utterly by thy power the demonic malice and snares, O Most Pure One, and save me.[^7681]

*Troparion 2:* Enlighten by thy prayers, O Virgin Mother, my mind darkened by the murk of wicked thoughts, by the mist of pleasures, and by the darkness of transgressions, O thou who didst bring forth the Divine Light; and flash forth upon me the unceasing enlightenment of repentance and grace.[^7682]

*Troparion 3:* Direct me unto the divine paths, O Mother of the King of all, delivering me from the trackless paths of passions and sin, and guiding me unto incorruptible life; for thou art the cause of all good things and the salvation of mankind, saving from corruption and leading upward unto salvation.[^7683]

*Troparion 4:* Having borne as a young Child Him Who is before all ages, O Pure One: pray Him unceasingly for the world, O all-blessed Virgin Mother; and as a most pure Mother, entreat the remission of my sins, having ever great boldness toward thy Son, O Pure One.[^7684]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Ineffable wonder! He Who delivered the holy youths from the flame in the furnace is laid in the tomb a breathless corpse, for the salvation of us who sing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer.[^7685]

*Troparion 1:* When the Jewish assembly condemned Thee upon the Cross, beholding it the earth was shaken, the sun hid its light; and those who were in darkness beheld the light, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer.[^7686]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst endure reproach, O Savior, being beaten with a reed and crowned with thorns...[^7687]

...as a king, being crowned voluntarily, O Christ God, for the salvation of us who sing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer.[^7688]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye are bound, O all-praised martyrs, loath to loose the delusion of the devil; ye endure torments and unjust death, desiring life on high, and crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer.[^7689]

*Martyrikon 2:*[^7690] Ye stood in the midst of the flame, O all-praised martyrs, manifestly burning up delusion; and not being burned by the dew of divine grace, ye cried aloud, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer.[^7691]

*Theotokion:* Thou art crucified voluntarily, O unoriginate Son, cried aloud the Birthgiver of God, beholding Thee: I am now engulfed with sorrow of soul; Thou diest, Who grantest life unto those who sing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer.[^7692]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^7693]

*Troparion 1:* Confirm my soul upon the rock of the desires of my Creator, O pure Lady, having stilled by thy prayers the tempest of pleasures and the tumult of passions, and granting me divine tranquility and peace.[^7694]

*Troparion 2:* Grant me a shower of tears, O most pure Lady, washing away all defilement and the mire of passions from my soul, presenting me pure unto the Creator, and delivering me from all torments.[^7695]

*Troparion 3:* Having acquired thee as intercessor and unconquerable confirmation, O blameless and pure Lady: may we appear superior to the assaults and snares of the enemy, and be delivered from dread and eternal condemnation.[^7696]

*Troparion 4:* The night of my wicked sins holds me fast, and the mist of lawlessness and the depth of evils, lo, covers me; lead me forth from the pit of sin, O Birthgiver of God and intercessor of the faithful.[^7697]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Be astonished in fear, O heaven, and let the foundations of the earth be shaken; for lo, He Who dwells in the highest is numbered among the dead, and is lodged as a stranger in a narrow tomb. Ye youths, bless Him; ye priests, hymn Him; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7698]

*Troparion 1:* Thou drinkest the cup which Thou didst desire, O Christ, through the passion of the Cross, pouring forth unto me fountains of forgiveness from Thy life-bearing ribs, unto me who died for the sake of the rib. Hymning Thee...[^7699]

...I sing: Ye youths, bless Him; ye priests, hymn Him; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7700]

*Troparion 2:* When the lawless people condemned Thee to be crucified, O Jesus, in the midst of transgressors, O Lord, Giver of life, the earth was shaken, and all sang in fear: Ye youths, bless Him; ye priests, hymn Him; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7701]

*Martyrikon 1:* Honorable before God was your death, O sufferers; for partaking of many passions, ye showed yourselves to be communicants of the honorable passions of Christ, crying aloud with one mind: Ye youths, bless Him; ye priests, hymn Him; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7702]

*Martyrikon 2:* Truly your weapons against the array of enemies were not carnal, O martyrs, but hope, faith, and truth; through which having attained unto God, ye were joined unto the angelic choirs, crying aloud unto the Master: Ye youths, bless Him; ye priests, hymn Him; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^7703]

*Theotokion:* O Most High, unoriginate Son, enduring spitting, reproaches, and the Cross, being beaten mockingly with a reed, the Birthgiver of God cried aloud: I glorify Thy longsuffering, Whom the youths bless, the priests hymn, and the people supremely exalt unto all ages.[^7704]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^7705]

*Troparion 1:* Having given birth to Christ the Light, through thy luminous entreaties and prayers, O Virgin, fill with the unwaning Light thy servants who in faith piously hymn thee; for by thy prayer and thine intercession thou art mighty to save whomsoever thou wilt.[^7706]

*Troparion 2:* Evil troubles and sorrows of hades have found me, and severe necessities and sicknesses cruelly hold and destroy me; but I invoke thy help, O Virgin: do thou deliver me from these troubles, directing me unto true tranquility.[^7707]

*Troparion 3:* Overthrow the hostile hosts by thy might, O good Birthgiver of God, of those who war against me and torment me exceedingly, and rejoice at my ruin: guarding, preserving, and delivering me from their wicked frenzy and cruel craftiness.[^7708]

*Troparion 4:* Behold my sorrow, O Pure One, which the multitude of sins hath brought upon me; and quench the furnace of my fiery passions with the dew of thy divine prayer, that I may hymn thy fervent protection, O Maiden, and supremely exalt thee unto all ages.[^7709]

*My soul magnifies the Lord.*[^7710]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Weep not for Me, O Mother, beholding in the tomb the Son Whom thou didst conceive in the womb without seed; for I shall arise and be glorified, and as God I shall exalt in glory unceasingly those who magnify thee in faith and love.[^7711]

*Troparion 1:* That Thou mightest deliver from corruption him who decayed through the sweet tasting of the tree, O only Savior, as Good One Thou didst will to be crucified in the flesh and put to death. We hymn unceasingly, O Jesus, Thy great compassion and Thy power.[^7712]

*Troparion 2:* O how didst Thou endure pains, being stretched out upon the Cross, snatching me from cruel pains, O Savior? How, being crowned with thorns, didst Thou burn up all the thorns of passions? How, having been given vinegar to drink, didst Thou mingle for us the cup of gladness?[^7713]

*Martyrikon 1:* Being all oppressed and cast into prisons, and cruelly dismembered in your limbs, O Sufferers, and stretched harshly upon wheels, and given over to be eaten by wild beasts, ye denied not Christ, the Master of all.[^7714]

*Martyrikon 2:* Brighter than the sun's rays shines the all-radiant and celebrated memory of the sufferers; and it ever enlightens the souls of the pious, and drives away the darkness of passions and temptations, and scatters the profound demonic gloom.[^7715]

*Theotokion:* Thou bearest Him Who bears all things, and holdest as an infant upon thine arm Him Who delivered us from the hand of the adversary enemy, O most pure Lady; and thou seest Him lifted up upon the wood of the Cross, Who delivered us from the pit of malice.[^7716]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^7717]

*Troparion 1:* Utterly from the heart I offer myself unto thee, and place all my hope in thee: through thee may I find deliverance from sins; through thee may I appear a partaker of the future kingdom and glory, O pure Birthgiver of God.[^7718]

*Troparion 2:* Having found thee as intercessor and protection, hope and wall, refuge, purification, and deliverance from cruel things, O Most...[^7719]

...Pure One, we are saved from assaults and troubles, sicknesses and passions, and unceasingly honor thy power.[^7720]

*Troparion 3:* Firm salvation and certain hope, who didst make heavenly the human race, who didst pour forth divine rain upon us, O Virgin Birthgiver of God: save us from passions and troubles and from eternal fire.[^7721]

*Troparion 4:* Having ineffably given birth to the supremely unoriginate Word of the Father, the Only-Begotten Son and God: pray Him unceasingly, O pure Maiden, that they may be delivered from troubles and from every threat, who magnify thee in faith and love.[^7722]

*It is truly meet. Exaposteilarion and the rest.*[^7723]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*[^7724]

*Sticheron 1:* In the Cross have I hope, and boasting therein I cry aloud: O Lord, Lover of mankind, cast down the pride of those who do not confess Thee to be God and man.[^7725]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord... And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^7726]

*Sticheron 2:* Being hedged about by the Cross, we oppose the enemy, fearing neither his craftiness nor his entrapment; for the proud one hath been brought to naught and trampled down upon the Tree by the power of Christ Who was crucified.[^7727]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct the works of our hands upon us; yea, direct the work of our hands.*[^7728]

*Martyrikon:* O Lord, in the commemoration of Thy saints, all creation keeps festival; the heavens rejoice with the angels, and the earth is glad with men. By their prayers have mercy upon us.[^7729]

*Glory... both now... Stavrotheotokion:* I was indeed nailed as a man upon the Tree, and was put to death, and was laid in the tomb as dead; but as God in glory, O Mother, pure Virgin, I rise again on the third day.[^7730]

*It is good to give praise. Trisagion and the rest.*[^7731]

*On Friday, The Beatitudes, Tone 6:*[^7732]

*Troparion 1:* Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom, and save me as the only Lover of mankind.[^7733]

*Troparion 2:* Having spread forth Thy palms upon the Tree, Thou didst embrace the nations who hymn Thy tender compassion, O Christ our God.[^7734]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast nailed upon the Tree voluntarily, and by Thy power, O Christ, Thou didst utterly shatter the stings of the proud one...[^7735]

...O Christ, Thou didst utterly shatter.[^7736]

*Troparion 4:* Delighting in torments, O passion-bearers, ye were counted worthy of the delight of Paradise, praying unceasingly for the world.[^7737]

*Glory: Triadikon:* Deliver us from all torment, O Father, and Son, and Spirit Who shares the throne, Thy servants, O Lord.[^7738]

*Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee stretched out upon the Cross, the Virgin sighed with tears; by her prayers save us, O Lord.[^7739]

## Saturday: All Saints and the Departed[^7740]

*On Friday Evening.*[^7741]

*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Martyrs, Hierarchs, and Venerables, Tone 6:*[^7742]

*Tone 6, Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Всѐ ѿло́жше):*[^7743]
